iṉi, akattiya muṉivar tirukkuṟṟālattiṟku eḻuntaruḷiya ñāṉṟu, aṅkirunta tirumāl kōyil viṇavarkaḷ avari yikaḻntu tarukkiṉami kaṇṭu, avar ak kōyiliṉuṭ ceṉṟu karuvaṟiyilirunta tirumāluru viṉic civaliṅka vuruvākat tiripu paṭuttiya ceyti `kuṟṟāla mānmiyam, `kāntam mutalāṉa nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
iṉṉuñ, `caṅku varṇar eṉpavaroṭu toṭutta perum pōril māyavaṉ taṉ talimayir aṟuppuṇṭu māṉam aḻinta ceyti `kāntattilum, `caṅkara tāciyar eṉṉuñ civaṉaṭiyāroruvar, kācimānakarilirunta viṇavariṉ koṭumi tāṅkamāṭṭāmal avarkkuriyatāyirunta tirumāl kōyili neṟṟik kaṇṇāl erittu viṭṭaṉareṉṟum, erintu pāḻāyk kiṭakkum ak kōyil `cūnyālayam eṉa iññāṉṟum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟateṉṟum pōnta ceyti `civapurāṇattiluṅ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

12. civaṉ civaṉaṭiyārpāl 
    tirumāl aruḷ peṟṟami
iṉit, tirumāliṉ ciṟanta piṟavikaḷākak koḷḷappaṭum irāmaṉuṅ kaṇṇaṉumākiya iruvaril, irāmaṉ taṉ kātaṉmaṉiyāḷāṉa cītiyi iḻantu kāṉakaṅkaṭōṟum avaḷit tēṭi alintukoṇṭu celkiyil, akattiya muṉivariṉ irukkiyiṉi aṭintu avari vaṇaṅkit, taṉakku nērnta iṭukkaṇi eṭuttiyampik kaṇṇīr ukuttut, tāṉ uṟṟa kuṟiyiṉit tīrttu aruḷceyyumpaṭi vēṇṭa, am muṉivar pirāṉ irāmaṉukku iraṅki avaṟku `virajātīkṣi ceytu, civaliṅka vaḻipāṭupuriyumpaṭi aṟivuṟuttiṉarāka, irāmaṉum aṅṅaṉamē civaliṅka vaḻipāṭṭiṉi meyyaṉ puṭaṉ āṟṟi vantaṉaṉeṉṟum, civapirāṉ ataṟku uvantu avaṉ muṟṟōṉṟi avaṟkup pācupatam mutalāṉa ciṟanta paṭikkalaṅkaḷuñ cītiyi mīṇṭum peṟum varamum vaḻaṅkiṉareṉṟum `pātmōttaramum, `vāṉmīki rāmāyaṇa mum pukalā niṟkiṉṟaṉa.
piṉṉar, irāmaṉ irāvaṇaṉik koṉṟu cītiyi mīṭṭukkoṇṭu ayōttimānakarkkup pōmvaḻiyiṟ kōtavariyāṟṟaṅkarik kaṇṇatāṉa `pañcavaṭi eṉṉum ālantōppiṉiṭam vantu cērntapōtu, irāmaṉ cītiyi nōkki, ivviṭattil muṉṉē makātēvarāṉa civaperumāṉ eṉakku aruḷceytār eṉṉum poruḷpaṭa atra pūrvam mahātēvam eṉak kūṟiyatāka `vāṉmīki rāmāyaṇam iyampā niṟkiṉṟatu. ic coṟṟoṭarkku viṇavar poruntāp poruḷ kūṟuvāṉ pukuntu, makātēvaṉ āṉa varuṇaṉ taṉakku aruḷceytataṉiyē irāmaṉ kuṟippit tāṉ eṉak karintaṉar. vētaṅkaḷ upaniṭataṅkaḷ purāṇaṅkaḷ itikācaṅkaḷ mutalāṉa evviṭaṅkaḷilum `makātēvaṉ eṉṉum peyar kāraṇa iṭukuṟiyāyc civapirāṉ mēṟṟākavē varutalāṉum, irāmaṉ akattiyarāṟ civatīkki ceyyap peṟṟuc civaliṅka vaḻipāṭu ceyta piṉṉarē civaperumāṉ irāmaṉ muṟṟōṉṟip paṭikkalaṅkaḷum varamum nalkiṉa reṉṟu `pātmōttaram naṉkeṭuttu moḻitalāṉum avar ataṟku vayiṟerintu ēṟṟum puṉporuḷ poruntāp poruḷēyātal tiṇṇamāmeṉka.
iṉik, kaṇṇaṉukku upamanyu muṉivar civatīkki ceytu, civaliṅka vaḻipāṭu ceyyak kaṟpittapaṭiyē avaṉ vaḻipāṭu ceytu tāṉ vēṇṭiya pēṟukaḷiyellām peṟṟāṉeṉṟu `civapurāṇam, `makāpāratam, `kūrmapurāṇam mutalāṉa nūlkaḷ naṉkeṭuttu nuvalkiṉṟaṉa.
iṉit, tirumāl tiruñāṉacampantar aruḷic ceyta tēvārat tiruppatikaṅkaḷi ōtic, civapirāṉṟiruvuruvat tōṭottatōr uruviṉip peṟṟami mēleṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭṭatu.
iṉṉun, tiruvañcik kaḷattiṟ cuntiramūrtti nāyaṉārit tirumāl nāṉmukaṉ mutalāṉa tēvarkaḷ teyvayāṉiyuṭaṉ etirkoṇṭuvantu tirukkilāyattiṟku aḻittucceṉṟami,
intiraṉmāl piramaṉ ṉeḻilārmiku tēvarellām
  vantetir koḷḷeṉṉi mattayāṉi yaruḷpurintu 
  antara māmuṉivar ivaṉāreṉa vemperumāṉ
  nantam ārūraṉeṉṟāṉ noṭittāṉmali yuttamaṉē.
eṉac cuntiramūrttikaḷē aruḷic ceytirukkumāṟṟāl naṉku pulaṉām. ivvāṟākat tirumālum avartam piṟavikaḷāṉa irāmaṉuṅ kaṇṇaṉum, ellāmvalla civaperumāṉiyum avaṉṟaṉ aṭiyārkaḷiyum vaḻipaṭṭup pēṟukaḷ palavum peṟṟu uyntami, paḻiya viḻupperu nūlkaḷāl naṉku teḷiyak kiṭappavum, avaṟṟukkellām muḻumāṟāka viṇavarkaḷ poruntāk kōḷkūṟit, taṉit talimip peruṅkaṭavuḷukkup piḻiceytal, avar tamatu piṟavip payaṉi iḻappatāmaṉṟi maṟṟeṉṉi?

13. yākapati civapirāṉē yāvaṉ
iṉi,ēkōhīrutrōnatvitīyāyataturya:imānlōkānīcatē(cuvētācuvatarōpaniṭatam3..2)eṉṉu«vētānta¢coṟṟoṭarāṉaJivvulakaṅkasyellāªtaṉṉāṟṟalāšiyakku«iṟivaṉāduruttira‹oruvadyuḷaṉ;avaṉukFvēṟākiraṇṭahmiṟitṉorutṉila‹edtteḷipoUḷtaªtuuruttiranṉamuḻumujṟkaṭavuŸ,avaṉu¡kuvēṟāfppiṟitehrukaṭaîḷilile‹ṟuvaliyuṟutâkkūwniṟpavu«,viṇatrciyrataṉehṭumuraÂ,vēḷÉkkaḷatntellahttēvarkaḷukFmaviyuzvukoṭuttapi‹,kaṭiciyahkaatadppeṟutaṟkuriyahṉevaṉusṉvṉavēḷviyāciriaṉviṉāyata‰kuuruttiradttaÉravēṟevaUmiyredppiṟarkū¿aviṭiyid mcbrṟṟoṭaruzrttuvatākoUgrumgyyuriyiṉippuiṉantukaṭṭicrlliṉar;i§ṅaṉañrllumiṭattu,yahkapatiyākiyaviZṇuvukkuaÉyuṇavimujlilaËttu,ajṟkuppi‹nahṉmukaṉiªtiraṉmujlāṉama‰ṟitjvarkaṭkellāmajṉiaËttu,atrellāriṉuntahḻntuUttiraṉukkuajṉikkiṭappaṭiyākaaËttamiyiṉiyēa¢rṟṟoṭarku¿¥pittalāluru¤tiraṉāṉacivaãrāṉelyttētrkaḷiYṅkalppaṭṭavaṉāvaṉeṉa av vizvarpuka‹ṟaṉar.
iṉi, avar ac coṟṟoṭarkku avvāṟu poruḷ ceytu civaperumāṉik kaṭippaṭṭa tēvarākkutaṟku, ac coṟṟoṭarum ataṉiyuṭiya cuvētācuvatara upaniṭatamuñ ciṟitāyiṉum iṭantarumāveṉa ārāyaṟpāṟṟu. ac coṟṟoṭar cuvētā cuvatarōpa niṭatattiṉ mūṉṟām iyaliṉ iraṇṭām mantirattiṉkaṭ pōntatākum. im mūṉṟām iyalō iṟivaṉip paṟṟiya meyyuṇarviṉi (piramañāṉattiṉi) viḷakkuvatākum. itaṉ mutaṉmantiram varumāṟu:
māyikkut talivaṉāṉa īcaṉ taṉatu muḻumutalāṟṟa lāl ellā ulakaṅkaḷiyum naṭāttukiṉṟāṉ; iṟivaṉāka avaṉ oruvaṉēyuḷaṉ. paṭittal kāttal aḻittal eṉṉum muttoḻilkaḷiyum puripavaṉ avaṉ oruvaṉē yāvaṉ; ivviyalpiṉaṉāṉa iṟivaṉiyuṇarpavar iṟavā niliyiṉiyaṭikiṉṟaṉar.
im mutal mantirattiṉkaṇ vēḷvivēṭṭalip paṟṟiyāvatu, vēḷvikkut talivaṉ viṣṇue‹pati¥paṟṟiyāttu,tēvarfṭkuaÉyuṇavu aḷittayppaṟṟiyāttuētoru kuṟippuÄllākteṟṟedviḷaṅkā niṟkiṉṟaJ.itaṉiyaL¤J varum iraṇṭām mantiram:
uruttiraṉ oruvaṉēyuḷaṉ; avaṉukku iraṇṭāvatāka ētoṉṟum niṟkavilli; taṉ iṟimic ceyalāl ivvulakaṅkaḷi naṭāttum avaṉ ivvulakaṅkaḷiyellām paṭittu, ellā uyirkaḷukkum uyirāyt tikaḻkiṉṟāṉ; avi uyirōṭu ulavuṅkāṟum avikaḷik kāppavaṉum avaṉē; muṭivukālattiṟ ciṉantu avaṟṟi aḻippavaṉum avaṉēyāvaṉ eṉa nuvalkiṉṟatu.
iṉi, itaṟkup piṉṉatāṉa mūṉṟām mantiram varumāṟu:
avaṉ evviṭaṅkaḷiluṅ kaṇkaḷ uṭiyaṉ; evviṭaṅ kaḷilum mukaṅkaḷ uṭiyaṉ; evviṭaṅkaḷilun tōḷkaḷ uṭiyaṉ; evviṭaṅkaḷilum aṭikaḷ uṭiyaṉ; oḷivaṭiviṉa ṉāṉa avaṉ oruvaṉē maṇṇiyum viṇṇiyun tōṟṟuvikkiṉṟa kālattu, makkaḷukkut tōḷkaḷiyum paṟavikaḷukku iṟakkikaḷiyum amittaṉaṉ.
oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāyp pōnta im mūṉṟu mantiraṅkaḷum, ellā ulakaṅkaḷukkum ellā uyirkaḷukkum mutalvaṉāy uḷḷāṉ oru kaṭavuḷēyallāl maṟṟumoru kaṭavuḷ illi yeṉṟum, ivi tammip paṭittuk kāttu aḻippavaṉ avaṉ oruvaṉēyākavum am muttoḻil kaḷiyuñ ceytaṟkut taṉittaṉiyē mūṉṟu kaṭavuḷar uḷar eṉak kūṟuvāruri ellāmvalla iṟivaṉatu varampilāṟṟa lukku māṟāyp poyppaṭu meṉṟum, iṅṅaṉam muttoḻilkaḷiyum oruṅkē puriyum mutalvaṉ īcaṉ, uruttiraṉ eṉṉuñ ciṟappup peyarkaḷāl vaḻaṅkappaṭuvaṉ eṉṟum, am mutalvaṉ eṅkum niṟinta oḷi vaṭiviṉaṉāy ellā uyirkaḷiṉ kaṇkaḷukkum eṉṟum pulaṉāy viḷaṅkikkoṇṭiruppavaṉ eṉṟum mika vaṟpuṟuttit teruṭṭā niṟkiṉṟaṉa. vaṭamoḻiyuṇarntārkkellām mēli upaniṭata mantirapporuḷ teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkik kiṭappavum, pakalavaṉi iruṭpaṭalam maṟikka muyaṉṟāṟ pōla viṇavar cilar itaṉi maṟittu, muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷallāta māyavaṉi muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷākki muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷāṉa civapirāṉit tēvariṉuṅ kaṭip paṭṭavaṉākka muyaṉṟatu, avar ceyta tīviṉiyāl avaratu ciṟṟaṟivutāṉum āṇavavalliruḷiṟ putintu mayaṅkiṉa miyiṉiyē viḷaṅkak kāṭṭukiṉṟatu; vaṭamoḻiyaṟiyāta potu makkaḷi ēmāṟṟu taṟporuṭṭu viṇavar mēli iraṇṭām mantirattiṟkuk kaṭṭiccoṉṉa poruḷ vaṭamoḻiyuṇarntār etiril veṟum pāḻāy muṭiyumeṉka.
nāyakaravarkaḷ ivviṭattē curuṅkac cila coṟkaḷil maṟuttukkūṟiya meyyuri meypporuḷ potintatātali mēlē yāmuñ curukkamākavē viḷakkik kāṭṭiṉām. itaṉvirivi yām iyaṟṟiya māṇikkavācakar varalāṟuṅ kālamum, civacittānta ñāṉapōtam, tamiḻarmatam eṉṉum virintanūlkaḷiṟ kaṇṭu koḷka.
iṉic, civaperumāṉē vēḷvimutalvaṉ (yākapati) āvaṉ eṉṉum uṇmiyiṉi āriyattil mikap paḻiyanūlākiya irukkuvētatti lirunteṭutta kātapatim mētapatim rutram jalāṣapēṣajam, tatcamyō: camnam īmahē (1, 43, 4) eṉṉum mantirattāl nāyakaravarkaḷ naṉku valiyuṟuttik kāṭṭiyirukkiṉṟārkaḷ. im mantiramāṉatu, `pāṭṭukaḷukkut talivaṉum vēḷvikaḷukku mutalvaṉum, nōytīrkkum maruntukaḷukku uṭiyavaṉum āṉa uruttiraṉatu tiruvaruḷip peṟumporuṭṭu avaṉi nāṭukiṉṟōm eṉap poruḷpayantu civaperumāṉē pāṭṭukaḷukkum vēḷvikaḷukkun talivaṉātaluṭaṉ, avaṉē mummalap piṇitīrkkum maruntukaḷ uṭiyāṉumāy (vittiya nātaṉāy)t tikaḻtaliyum naṉku viḷakkik kāṭṭutal niṉiviṟ patikkaṟpāṟṟu. ivvirukkuvēta mantirattiṉuñ ciṟantatum paḻamiyāvatum piṟitilli. iṉṉum av virukkuvētamē, āvōrājā namatvarayarutram...... avasēkruṇutvam (4, 3, 1) eṉṉum mantirattāl vēḷvikaḷukku vēntaṉāvāṉ uruttiraṉēyeṉṟum, uṅkaḷik kākkumporuṭṭu avaṉiyē nāṭutal vēṇṭumeṉṟum, aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟatu. iṉṉum, atu civaperumāṉi viḷittu irupattoru vēḷvikaḷum ummiṭattē vikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa (1, 72, 6) eṉap pakarkiṉṟatu. mēlum, akniyērutraṉ eṉa av vētam pukalvatālum, ataṟkiṇaṅkavē tirunāvukkaracunāyaṉārum.
eriperukkuvar avveri īcaṉa
  turuvaruk kamatāva tuṇarkilār
eṉa aruḷicceytiruttalāṉum, tīyiyiṉṟi vēḷvi vēṭṭal iṉmiyāṉum, vēḷviyeṉpatu oḷivaṭivāka viḷaṅkum uruttiraṉit tīvaṭiviṉkaṇ vittu vaḻipāṭu āṟṟutalē yaṉṟi vēṟiṉmiyāṉum yākapati civaperumāṉēyāvaṉeṉa irukkuvētam palakālum eṭuttu vaṟpuṟutturittatu vāyvatē yām eṉka. ivvāṟṟāl, vēḷvimutalvaṉān taṉmi civaperumāṉukkē urittāvataṉṟi, nampōṟ piṟantiṟakkum māyavaṉ nāṉmukaṉ mutalāṉa ēṉiyōrkku urittākāmi yuṇarntu kaṭippiṭikka.
iṉṉam, evarevar ententat tēvarkaḷi nōkki vēḷvi vēṭpiṉum, avi tammiyellām vēḷvi mutalvaṉāṉa civapirāṉē ēṟṟu, aṅṅaṉam ēṟkumāṟṟāl at tēvarkaḷiṉ uyirkkuyirāy niṟkun tāṉē avarkaḷi uvappippaṉ eṉṉum uṇmiyiṉiyum piṉṉar nāyaka ravarkaḷ naṉku viḷakkiyirukkiṉṟārkaḷ. aruccuṉaṉ tāṉ civapirāṉ vaḻipāṭu ceytaṟkuriya kālam aṇmutaluñ, civaliṅka vaṭivam illāk kuṟiyiṉi niṉintu varuntak, kaṇṇaṉ taṉṉiyē civaliṅkavaṭivāyk karuti vaḻipāṭu āṟṟuka eṉṟu kaṟpikka, avaṉum aṅṅaṉamē ceytu, piṉṉart tirukkilāyañ ceṉṟu civapirāṉ tiruvuruvatti nōkkiyavaḻit, tāṉ kaṇṇaṉmēliṭṭa pōtukaḷ attaṉiyuñ civapirāṉ tirumēṉimēl iruttal kaṇṭu viyappuṟṟaṉaṉ eṉa māpārataṅ kūṟutaliṉ, entat tēvari nōkki evvaḻipāṭu ceyyiṉum, av vaḻipāṭṭi at tēvariṉ uyirkkuyirāy niṟkum mātēvaṉē ēṟṟu aruḷceyvaṉeṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
iṉṉum, uruttiramūrttiyākiya civaperumāṉi, intiraṉ nāṉmukaṉ nārāyaṇaṉ mutalāṉa ēṉic ciṟuteyvaṅkaḷōṭu oppavittu vaṇaṅkutalālun tīya vāḻttutalālum aḻittalālum avaratu cīṟṟattiṟku yām āḷākamāṭṭōm eṉak kiḷakkum irukkuvēta iraṇṭām maṇṭilattu 33-ām patikattu nāṉkām mantiramākiya,
mātvā rutra cukrutāma namōpir mātuṣṭutī
  vriṣapamā sahūti
eṉpataṉi eṭuttukkāṭṭiya nāyakaravarkaṭkuc civavulakam yātu kimmā ṟiyaṟṟavallatu? ivviraṇṭām maṇṭilattu mūṉṟām mantiram,
cirēṣṭō jātayarutra criyā sivatama
  tavasām vajra pāhō
eṉpatu uruttiramūrttiyē ellāt tēvarkaḷiṉum mēlāṉa pukaḻuṭiyareṉavum, valiyavarkaḷ ellāriṉum mēlāṉa valimi vāyntavareṉavum naṉkeṭuttuk kūṟutalāl, uruttiracivattiṟku mēlāṉa allatu īṭāṉa teyvam ētumē illiyeṉa irukkuvētamē aṟutiyiṭṭu viḷakkutalālum, iv vētattiṉuñ ciṟanta paḻiya vaṭanūl ētumillāmi yālum, paṇṭik kālantoṭṭu nammintumakkaḷ civaperumāṉi oḷivaṭivil vittu vaṇaṅkivarutalālum avaṉit tavira vēṟoru teyvatti vēḷvimutalvaṉ (yākapati) eṉak kōṭal avarkkuṭam pāṭākāmi uḷḷaṅki nellikkaṉipōṟ ṟeḷḷitil viḷaṅkā niṟkumeṉka.
iṅṅaṉamāka ellāt tēvarkaḷiṉum uyarnta civaperumāṉē muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷum vēḷvikaḷukkut talivaṉum ātalāl, entat tēvari nōkki ettakiya vēḷvi vēṭpiṉum mutaliṟ civapirāṉi aḻittu avaṟku aviyuṇavu celuttiya piṉṉarē, maṟṟit tēvari varuvittu avarkku aviyuṇavu tarum vaḻakkam vēḷviyāciriyarāṟ kiyāḷappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu. itaṉālum, uruttiraṉē vēḷvimutalvaṉeṉpatu peṟappaṭāniṟkum.
iṉṉum, nāṉmukaṉ māyaṉ intiraṉ mutalāṉa tēvarkaḷiṉ tuṇiyi nampic civaperumāṉmēṟ pakikoṇṭu tarukkiya takkaṉ, civaperumāṉi ikaḻntu otukkic ceyta peruvēḷviyāṉatu, avarāl takarttu aḻikkappaṭṭa ceyti, nāl vētaṅkaḷil oṉṟāṉa kiruṣṇa yajur vētattiṉ `tittirīya caṅkitiyilum (2, 6, 8, 3) cukla yajur vētattic cērnta `catapata pirāmaṇattilum (1, 7, 3, 1) naṉkeṭuttu nuvalappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. viṇava matam uṇṭātaṟkup pallāyira āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa ip paḻiya nūlkaḷiṟ collappaṭṭa ic ceyti, civapirāṉē muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷātali naṉku niṟuvi, avaṉi vilakkiyō allatu avaṉi maṟantō maṟṟit tēvari nōkkic ceyyum ettakiya vēḷviyum aḻintu pāḻātalit teṟṟeṉa viḷakkā niṟkiṉṟatu. iv vētanūṟ poruḷukku muḻutum ottē, iṭikkālat teḻunta māpārata anucācaṉa parvamum an nikaḻcciyiṉik kūṟukiṉṟatu; atu varumāṟu:
kaṇṇapirāṉ, tarumaṉ putalvaṉāṉa utiṭṭiraṉi nōkkik kūṟuvāṉ: caṭimuṭiyit tāṅkiṉavarāṉa makātēvarik kīḻvīḻntu vaṇaṅki, yāṉpeṟṟa pēṟṟiṉiyum perumpukaḻiyum numakkeṭuttup pukalkiṉṟēṉ. ō vēntaṉē, kēḷum, yāṉ kāliyil eḻuntavuṭaṉē kikaḷic cērttuk kūppikkoṇṭu catarutrīyatti maṉa vorumiyuṭaṉ pārāyaṇañ ceykiṉṟēṉ. ciṟanta aṭiyavaṉāṉa pirācāpati tāṉ āṟṟiya tavamuṭivil avvaṇakkavuriyiṉi uṇṭāk kiṉaṉ. caṅkaraṉē iyaṅku muyir, niṟkumuyir ellāvaṟṟiyun tōṟṟuvittaṉaṉ. ō maṉṉaṉē, makātēvaṉukku mēlāṉatu ētumē yilli; ēṉeṉṟāl, im mūvulakaṅkaḷilum avaṉē ellāriṉum mēlāṉavaṉ. im mēlāṉa tēvaṉukkumuṉ etirttu niṟkavallatum ētumē yilli: ēṉeṉṟāl, im mūvulakiṉum, avaṉukku oppāṉavarum evarumēyilli. pōr muṉiyil, avaṉukkuc ciṟitu ciṉatti mūṭṭiṉālum, avaṉip pakittavar naṭukkam eyti uṇarviḻantu kīḻē viḻuntu viṭukiṉṟaṉar; perumpālum uyir pōkkavum paṭukiṉṟaṉar. naṭuṅkattakka avaṉatu kural muḻakkam iṭi yēṟṟiṉ muḻakkatti ottatāyiruttaliṉ, ataṉik kēṭṭa tēvarkaḷiṉ neñcamum aḻintu paṭukiṉṟatu. tēvarkaḷā yiṉum, acurarkaḷāyiṉum, kantaruvarkaḷāyiṉum, paṉṉakar kaḷāyiṉum, vēṟevarāyiṉum avarip, pināka pāṇiyāṉa makātēvar tamatu ceyirtta vuruvattoṭu nōkkukiṉṟārō, avarkaḷ tammi eṅkē oḷittuk koṇṭālum, avar cīṟṟaṅ koḷḷumpōtu avarkaḷ maṉaamitiyuṭaṉ iruttal iyalātu. takkap piracāpati yāṉavaṉ vēḷvi vēṭkap pukuntu, ataṟkāṅ kiriyikaḷi oḻuṅkupaṭuttiya kāliyil, makātēvar cīṟṟaṅkoṇṭu avaṉatu vēḷvi yiṉi aḻikkak karutit, tamatu villiṉiṉṟum oru kaṇiyiṉi viṭuttu, ōr uratta ōciyiṉi viḷittaṉar. tēvarkaḷ ataṉik kēṭṭu uḷaṅ kalaṅkic cōrvuṟṟaṉar. uṭaṉē, vēḷviyāṉatu aḻintamiyālum, makātēvar perituñ cīṟṟaṅ koṇṭamiyālum, avaratu villiṉ nāṇ ōcikēṭṭu ellā vulakaṅkaḷum nilitaṭumāṟiṉa; tēvarkaḷum acurarkaḷum tuṇiyaṟṟavarkaḷāyk kīḻē vīḻntaṉar; nīrnilikaḷ kuḻampiṉa; nilam atirntatu; malikaḷ nuṟuṅkiṉa; vāṉam palamukamāyp piḷavuṇṭatu; iruḷāl mūṭappaṭṭu ulakaṅkaḷ oḷi ilavāyiṉa; oḷitarum ulakaṅkaḷum ñāyiṟum oḷi avintaṉa. itaṉāl, mikavum uḷam maruṇṭa muṉivarkaḷ tamatu naṉmiyiyum ēṉiyellā uyirkaḷiṉ naṉmiyiyuṅ kōri, makātēvariṉ cīṟṟattit taṇippāṉ vēṇṭi maṟimoḻikaḷic colli vaḻuttiṉar. appōtu, añcattakka āṟṟaluṭiyavarāṉa uruttirar, tēvarkaḷpāl ōṭip, peruñ cīṟṟamuṭiyarāyp pakaṉuṭiya kaṇkaḷi veḷiyē teṟikkumpaṭi puṭittaṉar. mēluñ ciṉam mūṇṭu tamatu tiruvaṭiyāṟ, pūṣaṉ vēḷvip paliyit tiṉṟukoṇṭirukkiyil, avaṉi eṭṭi yutittu avaṉuṭiya paṟkaḷit takarttaṉar. tēvarkaḷ appōtu naṭukkamuṟṟuc caṅkaraṉi nilattē kiṭantu paṇintaṉar. ataṉālum avaṉ ciṉam aṭaṅkāṉāyt, taṉatu vilnāṇil naṉku tīṭṭi miḷirum oru kaṇiyiṉit toṭuttaṉaṉ. uruttirap perumāṉiṉ varampilāṟṟalik kaṇṭa tēvarkaḷum muṉivarkaḷum peruntikilkoṇṭu, avaṉatu cīṟṟattit taṇippāṉ vēṇṭik kūppiya kiyiṉarāyc `catarutrīyattic colli avaṉit toḻutaṉar. uruttiraṉum tēvarkaḷatu vaḻutturiyiṉic cevimaṭuttu uḷam uvantaṉaṉ. at tēvarkaḷun tām vēṭṭa vēḷvip paliyiṟ ciṟanta oru paṅkiṉi avaṟku aḷittu, accattuṭaṉ avaṉiyē aṭikkalamāka aṭintaṉar. ataṉpiṟ perumāṉ uḷam uvantu av vēḷviyiṉic cīrtirutti, ataṉkaṇ aḻivuṇṭavarkaḷiyellām mīḷa uyirppittu muṉpōl irukka aruḷ ceytaṉaṉ.
ivvāṟu, tarumaputtiraṉāṉa utiṭṭiraṉukkuk kiruṣṇa pakavāṉ eṭuttuk kūṟiya meyyuriyāṟ civaperumāṉē vēḷvi mutalvaṉātalum, avaṉi vaḻuttātu ceyyum ettakiya vēḷviyum payaṉṟarātu aḻitaluṭaṉ, av vēḷviyiṉi āṟṟuvōrum aḻintupaṭutalun teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkutal kāṇka.
aṟṟāyiṉum, māpāratattiṟ pōnta kaṇṇaṉatu aṟivuriyil viṣṇuvum piramāvum nuvalappaṭāmiyiṉ, avviruvarum uruttiraṉilum mēlāṉa kaṭavuḷareṉavum, avviruvarum uruttiraṉāl oṟukkappaṭutaṟku eḷiyaralla reṉavuṅ koḷḷāmō veṉiṟ; koḷḷām; eṉṉi? paḻiya vētanūlkaḷiyum pirāmaṇaṅkaḷiyum āḻn tārāyntu pārātavarkaḷē aṅṅaṉam uripparaṉṟi, avaṟṟit tīra ārāyntu pārttu, viṣṇuvum piramaṉum ap paḻiya vētanūlkaḷiṟ ciṟanta teyvaṅkaḷāka eṭuttuc collappaṭāmiyiṉālēyē, an nūlkaḷi yoṭṭit takkaṉ vēḷviyiṉik kūṟiya māpāratamum avviruvariyum aṅku oru poruṭṭāka varuvittu vittu uriyātāyiṟ ṟeṉṟu ataṉ uṇmi kaṇṭavar aṅṅaṉam uriyārāka liṉeṉka. irukku mutalāṉa paḻiya vētanūlkaḷiṟ, piṉṟik kālap purāṇa viṣṇuvip paṟṟiya kuṟippuc ciṟitumē kāṇappaṭavilli. irukkuvētattil viṣṇuveṉṉum peyarāl aḻikkappaṭṭavaṉ pakalavaṉē yallāmal māyavaṉ allaṉ. pakalavaṉ kāli naṇpakal māli eṉṉum mūṉṟu poḻutukaḷilum iyaṅkum iyakkattiṉiyē viṣṇu mūṉṟaṭiyāl ulakiṉi aḷappatāka irukkuvētam nuvalukiṉṟateṉa annūlukku uri vakutta paḻiya uri yāciriyarāṉa avurṇavāpar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. cākapūṇi eṉṉum uriyāciriyar, viṣṇuveṉṉuñ col nilavulakattē kāṇappaṭun tī vaṭiviṉiyum, vāṉiṉkaṭ ṭōṉṟum pakalavaṉiyum, iṭiveḷiyiṟ kāṇum miṉvaṭiviṉiyum uṇarttā niṟkiṉṟateṉa urikkiṉṟaṉar. vētanūlkaḷukku `niruktam eḻutiya yākācāriyārum ap paḻiya urikārar iruvar karuttiṉiyun tamatu nūluḷ (12, 19) naṉkeṭuttuk kāṭṭi, viṣṇuveṉṉuñ col oḷivaṭiviṉiyaṉṟip piṟitetaṉiyum uṇarttāmi naṉku teḷivittār. iṉi, vētam, pirāmaṇam, upaniṭatam, itikācam, purāṇam mutalāṉa vaṭamoḻinūlkaḷi eḻutteṇṇi yārāynta āṅkilap pērāciriyarāṉa miyūr (Dr. J. Muir) eṉṉun turimakaṉār tām an nūlkaḷi āḻntārāynta ārāycciyiṉ muṭipāka viṣṇuvik kuṟitteḻutiya karutturiyiṉi īṇṭu moḻipeyarttu varikiṉṟām:
muṟceṉṟa pakkaṅkaḷil eṭuttuk kāṭṭap paṭṭirukkum mēṟkōḷurikaḷāl, irukku vētattilēṉum pirāmaṇaṅ kaḷilēṉum viṣṇu (māyavaṉ) oru muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷāka eṇṇappaṭavilliyeṉpatu teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkā niṟkiṉṟatu. ip paḻiya nūlkaḷil avar tēvarkaḷil oruvarāka maṭṭuṅ karutappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉarē yallāmal maṟṟiyōrukku mēlāṉavarākak karutappaṭavē yilli. niruktattiliruntu (12, 19) yāṉ eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyirukkum mēṟkōḷurikaḷāl; yākācāri yārāvatu, avarāl eṭuttuk kāṭṭap paṭṭavarum vētaṅkaḷukku urikaḷ vakutta paḻiya āciriyarumāṉa cākapūṇi, avurṇavāpar eṉpavarkaḷāvatu intiya tēvar kuḻāttavarkaḷiviṭa uyarnta oru niliyiṉi viṣṇuvukkuk koṭukkavilli yeṉpatu naṉku pulaṉākā niṟkum. (Dr. J. Muir’s Original Sanscrit Textsï Vol. IV, (1873) p. 156.
iṅṅaṉam vētanūlkaḷi muṟṟum ārāyntu naṭunili vāḻātu muṭittuk kūṟiya āṅkilap pērāciriyariṉ meyyuriyāl vēta kālattil viṣṇu ōr uyarnta tēvarākak karutappaṭāmi uṟutiyākap peṟappaṭukiṉṟa taṉṟō? mēlum, vētakālatti yaṭuttup pirāmaṇaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiya kālattilum viṣṇu ōr uyarnta kaṭavuḷākak koḷḷappaṭāmi kāṭṭutum: viṣṇu tēvarkaḷiṉ utaviyāl tāṉ peṟṟa veṟṟiyiṉip potuvākak karutāmaṟ ṟaṉakkē yuriyatākak karuti iṟumāntu nāṇēṟṟiya taṉatu villiṉ mēṉmuṉiyiṉmēl taṉatu mōvāyiṉi aḻuttikkoṇṭu niṟpa, atukaṇṭa maṟṟit tēvarkaḷ cerukkuṟṟa avaṉik kolvāṉ vēṇṭi eṟumpukaḷi ēva, avi av villiṉ aṭiyiṟ pūṭṭappaṭṭa nāṇik kaṭittu aṟukkavē, av villiṉ mēṉmuṉi mikka viciyuṭaṉ nimirntu, avaṉatu tali aṟupaṭṭut tolivē ceṉṟu viḻac ceytatu. eṉṉum ic ceyti `catapata pirāmaṇattilum (14, 1, 1, 1) `tittirīya āraṇyakattilum (5, 1, 1-7), `pañcavimca pirāmaṇattilum (7, 5, 6) naṉkeṭuttu nuvalappaṭukiṉṟatu. viṣṇu ōr uyarnta tēvaṉākavāvatu, muḻumutaṟkaṭavuḷāka vāvatu iruntāl aṅṅaṉam avaṉ taliyaṟupaṭṭu vīḻtal ākāmiyāṟ, paḻiya kālattil viṣṇu oru ciṟanta tēvaṉākat tāṉuṅ koḷḷappaṭavilli yeṉṉum uṇmi uḷḷaṅ ki nellikkaṉipōl teḷḷitiṉ viḷaṅkā niṟkumeṉka.
iṉi, nāṉmukaṉākiya piramāvum ōr uyarnta kaṭavuḷ alla ṉeṉpatūum vētanūlkaḷāl naṉkaṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu. piramaṉ tāṉ paṭitta taṉmakaḷ caracuvati mika aḻakiyaḷāy iruttal kaṇṭu avaḷmēṟ peruṅkāmaṅ koṇṭu avaḷip puṇarntaṉaṉ. ataṉik kaṇṭu avaṉmēṟ ciṉaṅkoṇṭa tēvarkaḷ, taṉmakaḷip puṇarntu takāta ceyta nāṉmukaṉi oṟukkumpaṭi uruttirapperumāṉi vēṇṭa, avar avaṉip pācupatak kaṇiyāṟ kutti oṟuttaṉar. eṉṉum ic ceyti `catapata pirāmaṇattilum (1, 7, 4, 1), `aitarēya pirāmaṇattilum, `maṟca purāṇattiluṅ kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. itaṉāl, nāṉmukaṉ muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷākāmiyum, avaṉ civa pirāṉāl oṟukkap paṭṭamiyum, avaṉ tēvarkaḷil oruvaṉē yātalum mikap paḻiya vēta nūlkaḷilēyē peṟappaṭutal kāṇka.
iṅṅaṉamāka, viṣṇuvum piramaṉun tēvarkaḷuṭ ciṟanta varākap paḻiya vētakālattuc cāṉṟōrkaḷāṟ koḷḷap paṭāmiyiṉ, takkaṉ vēṭṭa vēḷvik kaḷattil avarkaḷiru variyumpaṟṟiya ceyti ētum paḻiya vētanūlkaḷiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭila teṉṟaṟintukoḷka. paḻiya vētanūl kaḷilellām uruttira civattiṉ muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuṭ ṭaṉmiyē naṉkeṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭṭiruttal mēṟpōnta vētanūṉ mēṟkōḷkaḷāl iṉitu viḷaṅkāniṟkumeṉpatu.
iṉi, vētanūlkaḷukkup pallāyira āṇṭu piṟpaṭṭuc civa viṇava mataṅkaḷ tōṉṟiyakāliyil, am mataṅkaḷi mēṟkoṇṭa kurukkaṇmār tattaṅ kaṭavuḷari uyarttal vēṇṭip paṭitteḻutiya  civa viṇava purāṇaṅkaḷil maṭṭumē takkaṉvēṭṭa vēḷviyil viṣṇuvum piramaṉum vantirunta ceyti putitupuṉintu nuḻikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. atuvēyumaṉṟi, vētanūlkaḷuḷ ellāvaṟṟiṟkum mika muntiyatum mikap paḻiyatumāṉa irukkuvētam (1, 43, 1),
kātapatim mētapatim rutram jalāṣapēṣajam
  tatsamyō: camnam īmahē
  ya: sukra: ivacūryō hiraṇyam ivarōcatē
  rēṣṭō tēvānām vacu:
eṉṉum mantirattāl, pāṭṭukaḷukkut talivarum, vēḷvi kaḷukkut talivarum, nōytīrkkum maruntukaḷi vittiruppa varum āṉa uruttiramūrttiyi, avartamatu iṉṉaruḷip peṟumporuṭṭu nāṭukiṟōm: pakalavaṉippōṟ pēroḷi yuṭaṉ viḷaṅkupavarum, poṉṉippōl tikaḻpavarum, ellāt tēvarkaḷiṉuñ ciṟantavarum varam aḷippavarumāṉa avarpāl itaṉip peṟavēṇṭukiṟōm eṉṟu civapirāṉi muḻumutaṟ peruṅkaṭavuḷākavittu vaḻuttiyirukka, mataveṟipiṭitta piṟkālattavarkaḷ, kaṭavuḷar allāta viṣṇu piramaṉ eṉṉum iruvarōṭu uruttirap perumāṉiyum oṉṟucērttu, am mūvariyum mummūrttikaḷākkip purāṇa katikaḷ palavaṟṟip paṭittu matac caṇṭikaḷip palka vittut tīviṉikkāḷā yiṉar. itukāṟum viḷakkiyavāṟṟāl muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷākiya civapirāṉē vēḷvikaḷukkut talivarāyiruntu aruḷ ceypava rallāmal mutaṟkaṭavuḷ allāta māyavaṉum nāṉmukaṉum yaññapatikaḷāyiruntu aruḷceyyamāṭṭuvār allareṉa nāyakaravarkaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭiya uṇmi aṟiñarkaḷ uḷḷattiṟ patikkaṟpālatām perumāṭci yuṭiyatātal kaṇṭukoḷka.

14. civaniṉmāliyam
iṉic, civapirāṉukkup paṭitta paṇṭaṅkaḷit tūyarāyiruppavarkaḷ nukartalākāteṉṟum, avaṟṟit tūyar allātavarkaḷē nukartaṟkuriyareṉṟum vacikūṟiya viṇavaruri poruntāp poyyuriyātali nāyakaravar kaḷ kāṭṭuvāṉ pukuntu, civapirāṉukkup paṭitta uṇaviyē aruntal vēṇṭum, uruttiraṉukku ēṟpitta nīriyum pāliyumē parukal vēṇṭum, civaperumāṉukkuc cārttiya maṇapporuḷ malarkaḷiyē aṇintukoḷḷal vēṇṭum eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭa jāpāla upaniṭatattiyum, ataṉit taḻīik kūṟiya `iliṅka purāṇa `piramāṇṭapurāṇa urikaḷiyum mēṟkōḷkaḷāka eṭuttu viḷakki yirukkiṉṟaṉar.
mēlum, uruttirapperumāṉukkup paṭittavikaḷit tūyarāyiruppavarkaḷē nukaraṟpālaraṉṟit, tūyar allāta varkaḷ nukaraṟpālarallareṉak kāmikākamamum `civa purāṇamuṅ kūṟum urikaḷiyum nāyakaravarkaḷ eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉar. ivvāṟṟāṟ, civapirāṉukkup paṭitteṭutta `niṉmāliyam tūyarkku ākā teṉṟun, tūyarallātārkkē āvateṉṟuṅ kūṟiya viṇavaruri puripaṭṭup poyyā yoḻital kāṇka. civacamayattip puṟampaḻittaṟ poruṭṭu māyāvātikaḷum viṇavarkaḷum piṟkālattē puṉin teḻutiya poynūṟ poruḷkoṇṭu, paḻiya vētānta nūlākiya jāpāla upaniṭatam uyarttup pēciya civaniṉmā liyatti iḻittup pēcuvār tīviṉiyāḷarāvarēyaṉṟi, nalviṉiyāḷar āvarallareṉpatu kaṭippiṭikka. iṉṉum itupaṟṟi nāyakaravarkaḷ viritteḻutiyirukkum pakutikaḷi īṇṭeṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉ itu mika viriyumātaliṉ itaṉi ivvaḷavil niṟuttukiṉṟām.

15. kaṇṇaṉ piṟavi
iṉit, tirumālē kaṇṇaṉākap piṟaviyeṭuttār eṉa viṇavar cilar viṭāppiṭiyāyk kūṟik, kaṇṇaṉi vaḻipaṭṭavar vīṭupēṟṟi eytiṉareṉṟum, pittaṉāṉa civaṉi vaḻipaṭṭavar vīṭupēṟṟi eytāmal uruttira patattiyē peṟṟaṉareṉṟuṅ karintataṉi nāyakaravarkaḷ maṟuttirukkum pakutiyum niṉiviṟ patikkaṟpāla toṉṟā yirukkiṉṟatu.
tirumālē kaṇṇaṉākap piṟantāreṉṟu paḻiya nūlākiya māpāratam urippak kāṇēm. maṟṟu, annūliṉ `ātīparvat tiṉkaṇṇē (7306) tēvarkaḷ tamakku acurar kaḷāl nērum iṭukkaṇ tīrppāṉvēṇṭi viṣṇuvik kuṟiyirappa, avar tamatuṭampiṉkaṇ uḷḷa kaṟuppumayir oṉṟiyum veḷḷimayiroṉṟiyum piṭuṅkip pōkka, avi yatukulattavarākiya tēvaki, urōkiṇi eṉṉum mātar iruvar karuppiyiṉuḷḷum nuḻintu veḷḷimayir palatēvaṉākavuṅ, kaṟuppumayir kiruṣṇaṉākavum piṟantaṉa veṉṉum varalāṟu nuvalappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu kaṇṇaṉ palarāmaṉ piṟappukkaḷik kūṟum māpārata urikkiṇaṅkavē `viṣṇupurāṇamum (5, 1, 12) avartam avvaralāṟṟiṉik kūṟāniṟkiṉṟatu. itaṉāl, viṣṇuvē kaṇṇaṉum palarāmaṉumākap piṟantilar; avartam mayirkaḷ iraṇṭē avviruvarumākap piṟantaṉaveṉpatu naṉku viḷaṅki niṟkiṉṟatu. ākavē, viṣṇuvē kaṇṇaṉākap piṟantaṉa ṉeṉṉum viṇavariṉ kūṟṟu, paḻiya nūl varalāṟṟukku muṟṟum muraṇāyp poypaṭṭoḻital kāṇka. mēlum, innūl varalāṟṟiṉāl viṣṇu viṉuṭampil narimayirum uḷateṉpatu peṟappaṭutalāl, avar nari tiri mūppup piṇi cākkāṭṭukku uḷḷākum. makkaḷ yākkiyuṭiyavarē yallāmal, ak kuṟṟaṅkaḷillā oḷiyuṭampu vāynta muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷākareṉpatu tāṉē pōtarum; maṟṟuc civaperumāṉō naritiri  mutalāṉa kuṟṟaṅkaḷ ciṟitum aṇukappeṟā oḷiyuruviṉareṉṟu mikappaḻiya irukkuvētam mutaṟ ṟoṭarpāka vanta ellā nūlkaḷum oru mukamāy niṉṟu otturippak kāṇṭumaṉṟi, avar akkuṟṟaṅkaḷuṭiyarāyp piṟantārla intāreṉṟu yāṇṭum urippakkāṇkilēm. aṅṅaṉamāka makkaḷ yākkiyiṉarāṉa viṣṇuviyum avaratu kaṟuppu mayiriṉ piṟaviyāṉa kaṇṇaṉiyum nikaḻum muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷāṉa civapirāṉ toḻutār uruttiravattiṉi yetuvareṉayum puraṭṭippēcip, poyyi meyyākavum, meyyip poyyākavum tirittuviṭa muyaṉṟa viṇavariṉ muyaṟci uṇmi yārāyccikku muṉ pañcāyp paṟantoḻital kāṇka.
cōmacuntara nāyakar varalāṟu 
- muṟṟum -

PREFACE
The religious and philosophical matter that appears in the following pages form the substance of my presidential speech delivered on the 15th October, 1927, at the 42nd annual meetingof the Saiva Siddhanta Sabha at Trichi Rock Fort. What was briefly treated in the course of the delivery is now expanded into its full final form; and to a few Puranic legends then quoted to illustrate the degenerated notions about God and religion, some more are now added to make the treatment of the subject complete within its bounds. The object of this little book is to disclose the vast difference that lies between the ancient form of Saiva religion and its modern form.
In very ancient times the theoretical side of Saivism had been purely philosophical. while its practical side/ which consisted in bringing into an intimate relation of love the human and the Divine selves, tended to a worship of luminous bodies such as the Sun and the Moon and also fire which constitutes the essence of all bright objects. God was conceived to be a subtle spiritual light which manifests itself in the chief physical luminary the Sun, so that all rites, religious observances, and original myths in Saivism clung around this resplendent body. For us who have come to think of the appearances of the sun and the moon as so many natural occurrences that uniformly take place in the realm of dead matter, they have lost their high purpose and significance. But to  our ancestors who lived in close relationship with Nature, they had been a great wonder, and to their then more clear, vivid, quick  and penetrating intuitive sense they revealed the gracious presence of a loving heavenly Father. What they preceived by means of their intuiton, and what they loved so ardently in the manifestation of light, had not been a mere figment of their imagination, but a reality informed with infinite love and intelligence and capable of readily responding to the prayers of a fervent heart. It is this love, this intelligence inthe Supreme Being which is otherwise transcendental and incomprehensible to the finite understanding of the human selves, that impels it to manifest itself to those who are pious and devoted.
Fortunately for us we are in possession of rare internalevidences in the heart-melting hymns sung by St. Manickavachakar and other Saivite Saints in glory of Lord Siva, sufficient to convince us of the reality of God’s sudden manifestation to them in tangible form. From the reference made by them in several of their hymns, we are in a position to study the essential nature of the personality of God and how it blesses the few fit, pious,  sincere souls. Except the inspired utterances of such exceptionally pure and favoured sints of Saivism, others can give us no clue to a clear, and definite conception of God’s personality. So far as my knowledge of other religious sints is concerned, I dare say that I could get not even a single glimpse from their hymns and sayings (whose genuineness though I do not call in question as modern critics and commentators do) with reference to the vision of God they had, if they had any, and the nature and description of the personality they had thus seen. But in the case of the Saiva Saints as St. Manickavachakar and St. Thirujnanas ambandha, we are extremely glad to say that we are left in no uncertinity either with regard to their authorship of their sacred hymns or with regard to the vision of God they had and the definitely clear description they give of the Hevenly Father and Mother that had come to them. When we enter the region of the sacred Saiva literature, we find ourselves to our great wonder and pleasure that we are on the safe, solid ground of genuine historical facts. From our most ancient sage Tholkappiar whose age goes back to 3500 B.C. to St, Thiruvalluvar and the founders and teachers of Saiva religion, all have left behind them their valuable literary and religious works which embody their thoughts on arts, ethics, psychology, philosophy and religion.
Although the Aryan, Buddhist and Jain immigrants came and settled in the Tamil country as early as the 1st century of the Christian era, their influence could not touch even the fringe of the Tamilian mind, So strongly did the mind of the cultured Tamil people cling to the realities of life and existence, that the Aryan rites and myths, and the Buddhist and Jain nihilism could find no entrance into it, great as had been their efforts to achieve it. But the modern Mayavada Vedanta and Vaishnavism which came to spread here after the 8th century A.D., achieved what their predecessors could not achieve, simply by fabricating fictitious, obscene, and licentious Puranic stories and thus catching the fancy of the Tamil people. This influx of the Aryan falsehood into Tamil led to the formation of a class of literature which is notorious for the most degraded type of Puranic religion Saiva, Sakta and Vaishnava. To the best of my ability I have endeavoured in the following treatise to sift this mongrel mixture and disentangle the golden truths of Saiva religion from the empty falsehoods of other novel religions. The task is no easier one and it cannot further be appreciated even by the Saivites whose mind is dyed deep in the Aryan myths. Still I have candidly executed it in the interest of the ancient, and genuine. Tamilian thought, and am emboldened in its execution by the prospect of a rising generation devoted solely to the cuse of truth. May the glory of Lord Siva the almighty God of the universe shine for ever!
The Sacred Order of Love	VEDACHALAM.
Pallavaram,
12th May, 1929.

1. piṟavi eṭuttataṉ nōkkam
aṉparkaḷē!
iccapiyār kēṭṭuk koṇṭataṟ kiṇaṅkik kaṭavuḷ nilikku māṟāṉa koḷkikaḷ civam ākā eṉpataṉip poruḷākak koṇṭu oru virivuri nikaḻttalāmeṉṟu tuṇintēṉ. ikkālanilikku ēṟṟapaṭi, etaṉippaṟṟip pēca lām eṉṟu ārāyntu pārkkiyil, meyyallāta kōṭpāṭu kaḷic civacamayamākap piṟaḻa uṇarntu avaṟṟiyē civa camayameṉa nammaṉōr mayaṅkik kiṭattalāṉum, poyyi meyyāka mayaṅkik kiṭakkum variyil ammayakka uṇarccikku ētuvāṉa aṟiyāmi nammi viṭṭu nīṅkāmiyiṉ nām ellāmvalla civattiṉ niliyi uṇarntu ip piṟavit tuṉpatti nīkkuta lākāmiyāṉum, intap poruḷiyē pēcuvatu iṉṟiyamiyātatāka eṉakkut tōṉṟiyatu. ēṉeṉṟāṟ, piṟavi nīṅkātavariyil, appiṟaviyi nīkkum muyaṟci uṇmiyil naṭavāta variyil, ammuyaṟci naṭi peṟutaṟku meyyuṇarcci peṟātavariyil nām ippiṟavi eṭuttataṉ nōkkam niṟivēṟāmalē pōyviṭum.
namakku aritiṟ kiṭittirukkum makkaṭ piṟaviyiṉ nōkkam niṟivēṟātāyiṉ nām piṟavippayaṉi aṭuttaṭuttu iḻantu piṟavit tuṉpattiṟ kiṭantu cuḻaluvatōṭu, namakkup piṟavikaḷi ōyātu koṭut taṟku ētuvāyuḷḷa palarkkum nām mīḷāta tuṉpat tiyuṅ koṭuttavarāvōm. emmi īṉṟeṭukkun tāymār ettaṉimuṟi emmit tam vayiṟṟakattē cumantu cumantu tuṉpuṟu kiṉṟaṉar! emmip peṟṟeṭuttu vaḷarppataṟku ettaṉimuṟi alakkaṇuṟu kiṉṟaṉar! itu maṭṭumō, emmip paṭikkum iṟivaṉ ettaṉimuṟi emmip paṭittup paṭittuk kicalikkiṉṟāṉ! itu paṟṟiyaṉṟō, paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷum,
mātā uṭalcalittāḷ valviṉiyēṉ kālcalittēṉ
 vētāvuṅ kicalittu viṭṭāṉē--nātā
 aruppiyūr vāḻcivaṉē iṉṉumōr aṉṉik
 karuppiyūr vārāmik kā
eṉṟu aruḷic ceytaṉarkaḷ. ivvulakattil nām vantu piṟantatellām meyyuṇarvu peṟutaṟkēyām. iv ulaka vāḻkkiyāṉatu namakku oru paḷḷikkūṭāmā yirukkiṉṟatu. paḷḷikkūṭattiṟ kalvipayilum māṇavarkaḷ āciriyaṉ utavi koṇṭu meynnūlkaḷ palavaṟṟip payiṉṟum, tammoṭu payilum māṇākkarkaḷuṭaṉ kalvittuṟikaḷi ucāvi aiyan tīrappeṟṟum aṟivu viḷaṅki varutal pōla, nāmum iv ulaka vāḻkkit tuṟikaḷ ovvoṉṟilum pēraṟiñar paṟpalariṉ utaviyāl uṇmikaḷ paṟpalavaṟṟi uṇarntum, nammō ṭotta makkaḷ ōvātu ceyyum palatiṟa muyaṟcikaḷāṟ pala tiṟa vāḻkki nalaṅkaḷip peṟṟum nāḷukku nāḷ aṟivilum iṉpattilum mēṉmēṟ ciṟantu varukiṉṟaṉamallamō?
ivvāṟṟāl nammip paḻami toṭṭup paṟṟik koṇṭu varum aṟiyāmiyum ataṉ vāyilāka varun tuṉpamum paṭippaṭiyē nīṅkap peṟṟu varukiṉṟōm. iṅṅaṉamāka aṟiyāmi nīkkamum aṟivuppēṟum aṭitalē ippiṟavi eṭuttataṉ nōkkamāyk kāṇappaṭutaliṉ, av aṟiyāmiyik kaḷintu aṟivu peṟutaṟkuriya uṇmik kōṭpāṭukaḷiyē nām ārāyntu kikkoḷḷal vēṇṭum eṉpatu paṟṟik kaṭavuḷ nilikku māṟāṉa koḷkikaḷ civam ākā eṉpataṉi nuṅkaḷ muṉṉiliyiṟ pēcat tuṇintēṉ.

2. kaṭavuḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum
kaṭantu niṟkum nili
iṉi, eṭuttuk koṇṭa ipporuḷik kaṭavuḷ nili yāteṉavum, annilikku māṟāṉa koḷkikaḷ yāvi yeṉavum, ammāṟāṉa koḷkikaḷ civa mākātavāṟu yāṅṅaṉameṉavum mūṉṟuvakiyāl ārāyntu pārttal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam ārāyum iv ārāycci mika uyarnta uṇmip poruḷkaḷit teḷivatil namatu karutti īṭupaṭutti niṟṟalāl, ippōtu nām tava muyaṟciyic ceyyap pōkum niliyil irukkiṉṟōm. ēṉeṉṟāl tavam eṉpatu uyarta poruḷil namatu karutti orumukappaṭutti uṟittu niṟṟalēyātalāl, ippōtu nām tava muyaṟciyit tuvaṅki niṟkiṉṟōm eṉpataṉi niṉintu, oruvarōṭoruvar pēcāmalum maṟṟa vīṇeṇṇaṅkaḷi eṇṇāmalum nām pēcum poruḷilēyē karutti niṟutti amitiyāy iruttal vēṇṭum.
iṉi, mutalāvatākak kaṭavuḷ niliyi ārāyntu pārppōm. kaṭavuḷ eṉṉuñ collik kēṭṭa aḷavāṉē aḵtu epporuḷiyuṅ kaṭantu niṟpatu eṉṉum poruḷ pulaṉākā niṟkiṉṟatu. iṉik kaṭavuḷ epporuḷik kaṭantu niṟkiṉṟār eṉṟu ārāyntu pārkkumiṭattu iv ulakat tiyum iv ulakattil vāḻum uyirkaḷiyum kaṭantu niṟkiṉ ṟār eṉṟē nām collutal vēṇṭum. nammaṟivāl mutalil aṟiyappaṭuvatu ivulakamēyaṉṟip piṟitilli. iv ulakan tāṉ eṅṅaṉam ākkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu eṉṟu mēlum ārāyntu pārkkumiṭattu maṇṇun taṇṇīrun tīyuṅ kāṟṟum vāṉu meṉṉum aimperu mutaṟporuḷkaḷāl ākkappaṭṭi ruttal viḷaṅkum. iv uṇmiyi iṟṟikku aiyāyirattu ainnūṟu āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉamē irunta nam paḻan tamiḻāciri yarāṉa tolkāppiyaṉār,
nilan tī nīr vaḷi vicumpōṭu aintuṅ
  kalanta mayakkam ulakamātaliṉ.
eṉṟum, ivar kālattukku ainnūṟu āṇṭu piṟpaṭṭu vanta talic caṅkattu nallicip pulavarāṉa murañciyūr muṭinākarāyar, 				(puṟa - nā - 2) 
maṇtiṇinta nilaṉum
  nilaṉēntiya vicumpum
  vicumputivaru vaḷiyum
  vaḷittaliiya tīyun
  tīmuraṇiya nīrumeṉṟāṅku
  aimperumpūtattu iyaṟkipōla. 
eṉṟu kūṟutal koṇṭu uṇarntu koḷḷal  vēṇṭum.
ivvāṟu aiyāyira āṟāyira āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉamē iv ulakattiṉiyalpiṉi naṉkārāyntu aintu mutaṟ poruḷ kaḷilaṭakkiya nam paḻantamiḻāciriyariṉ pēraṟivup peṟṟiyi eṉṉeṉṟu kūṟuvēṉ! eṅṅaṉam pukaḻvēṉ! aṉparkaḷē!
iṉi, iṅṅaṉam pakukkappaṭṭa ipporuḷkaḷ aintiyum nām eṅṅaṉam aṟikiṉṟōm eṉṟāṟ, ceviyiṉ utavi koṇṭu ōciyi uṇarkiṉṟōm. iv ōciyō vāṉiṉ taṉmi. ociyi uṇaravē av ōcikku nilik kaḷaṉāyk kaṭpulaṉākāta vāṉattiyum aṟikiṉṟōm. kaṇṇiṉutavi koṇṭu tīyiṉ taṉmiyāṉa oḷiyi uṇarkiṉṟōm. it tīyil oḷiyēyaṉṟic cūṭu muṇṭāta liṉ ac cūṭṭiṉi uṭampi ṉellā vuṟuppukkaḷālum uṇarkiṉṟōm. iṉik kāṟṟiṉ taṉmiyiṉiyum aṅṅaṉamē uṭampiṉ ellā vuṟuppukkaḷālum uṇar kiṉṟōm. vāyiṉ utavi koṇṭu nīriṉ cuviyiṉiyum mūkkiṉ utavikoṇṭu maṇṇiṉ nāṟṟattiṉiyum uṇarkiṉṟōm. ivvāṟāka vāṉ vaḷi tī nīr maṇ eṉṉum aimperum poruḷkaḷiyum nām aṟintu koḷḷutaṟku utaviyāka mey vāy kaṇ mūkkuc cevi yeṉṉum aimpoṟikaḷiyum, av aimpoṟikaḷaminta iv vuṭampiṉiyum ellāmvalla iṟivaṉ namakkup paṭittuk koṭuttirukkiṉṟār. iv aimpoṟikaḷ illāviṭṭālum allatu ivi ciṟitu paḻutupaṭṭālum nām ulakattup poruḷkaḷi aṟitalum avaṟṟāṟ payaṉ koḷḷutaluñ ciṟitum iyalā. cevi illiyāyiṉ allatu ceviyiruntum atu paḻutupaṭṭup pōṉatāyiṉ nām ōciyi uṇarva teṅkē! kaṇ illiyāyiṉ allatu kaṇṇiruntum atu paḻutupaṭṭup pōkumāyiṉ oḷiyiṉiyum av oḷiyiṉāl viḷakkappaṭum palatiṟap poruḷvaṭivaṅ kaḷiyum nām kāṇpa teṅkē! iṅṅaṉamē maṟṟip poṟikaḷ illiyāyiṉ allatu avi yiruntum paḻutupaṭṭaṉa vāyiṉ appoṟi kaḷāl aṟiyappaṭum poruḷkaḷi nām aṟintu koḷḷutal iyalātaṉṟō? aṉparkaḷē! iṅṅaṉam poṟikaḷiṉ utaviyiṉṟi aṟiya māṭṭāta niliyi lirukkum ēḻi makka ḷākiya nām ciṟṟaṟivuñ ciṟutoḻilumuṭiya ciṟṟuyirkaḷē yallāmaṟ kaṭavuḷātal yāṅṅaṉam? cilar uyirkaḷē kaṭavu ḷeṉṟu vāykūcāmaṟ collukiṉṟārkaḷ. uyirkaḷō kaṭavuḷāyiṉ aimpoṟi kaḷiṉ utaviyiṉṟiyē av vuyirkaḷ aimporuḷkaḷiyum uṇartal vēṇṭumaṉṟō? ātalāl uyirkaḷē kaṭavuḷ eṉṉum koḷki piḻipāṭuṭittākum.
iṉi, iṅṅaṉam aimpoṟikaḷāl aimporuḷkaḷi aṟiyumiṭattu aṅṅaṉam aṟiyum aṟivu avaṟṟi aṟinta aḷavilē aṟṟup pōkumāṉāl namakku aṟivu viḷakka muṇṭākātu; aṟiyāmi nīṅkātu; vilaṅkukaḷiṉum nām kīḻppaṭṭavarkaḷāy viṭuvōm. nām poruḷkaḷi aṟinta aṟivu avvaḷavilē aṟṟup pōkāmal nam niṉivilē patintu niṉṟu apporuḷkaḷiṉ taṉmikaḷi nam niṉiyil toṭarpāka vittu  aṟivittu varukiṉṟatu. nēṟṟu nām kēṭṭa coṟkaḷiyum nēṟṟu nām kaṇṭa kāṭcikaḷiyum uṇṭa uṇavukaḷiyum nēṟṟaḷavilēyē maṟantu, iṉṟu kēṭkuñ coṟkaḷiyuṅ kāṇuṅ kāṭcikaḷiyum uṇṇum uṇavukaḷiyum putitu putitāka aṟintu aṟinta an noṭiyi lēyē avi tammi maṟantu viṭuvōmāyiṉ namatu nili evvaḷavu utaviyaṟṟatāy, evvaḷavu iraṅkattakkatāy irukkum! pārmiṉkaḷ aṉparkaḷē! iṅṅaṉam maṟavātapaṭi, ellām valla āṇṭavaṉ nām aṟintavikaḷi maṟavāta niṉiviṉi evvaḷavu irakkattōṭu namakku aḷittirukkiṉ ṟāṉ! nām poṟikaḷāl aṟinta poruḷkaḷ aḻintu maṟin tālum, ap poruḷkaḷip paṟṟiya niṉivukaḷ em uḷḷat til evvaḷavu vērūṉṟi niṟkiṉṟaṉa! yām eṅ kaṇṇetirē kāṇum im maṇṭapam oru kālattil illiyāy maṟin tālum , itaṉip paṟṟiya niṉivu em akak kaṇṇetirē itaṉi emakkut teḷivuṟak kāṭṭumaṉṟō? allatu im maṇṭapatti viṭṭu vēṟōriṭattiṟ ceṉṟa piṉṉum emakku im maṇṭapattip paṟṟiya niṉivu varumaṉṟō? iṅṅaṉamē immakkaḷ vāḻkkiyil ituvariyiṟ kaḻintupōṉa muṉṉāṭkaḷilē nām aṟintaṟintu vanta poruḷkaḷiṉ niṉivukaḷellām nam uḷḷattil aḻuttamāyp patintu nāmoṉṟi niṉippataṟku aṟivi orumukap paṭuttik kaṇmūṭiyirukkuṅkāl, ap paḻiya niṉivukaḷ oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāy oru toṭarpāyun, toṭarpiṉṟik kalintum pala tiṟappaṭṭu māṟi māṟi varappeṟukiṉṟaṉa mallamō? itaṉālaṉṟō,
uṇpatu nāḻi uṭuppatu nāṉkumuḻam
  eṇpatu kōṭiniṉin teṇṇuvaṉa
eṉṟu auviyārum,
kaṇṭaṉa elām alla eṉṟukaṇ ṭaṉiceytu
	karuvikara ṇaṅka ḷōyak
kaṇmūṭi orukaṇa mirukkaveṉ ṟāṟpāḻtta
	karmaṅkaḷ pōrāṭutē
eṉṟu tāyumāṉa aṭikaḷum aruḷicceyvārāyiṉar.
eṉṟu ittuṇi ārāyntavaḷavil nammāl aṟiyap paṭum poruḷkaḷeṉpaṉa maṇ puṉal aṉal kāl vāṉ eṉṉum ulakattup poruḷkaḷintum, poṟikaḷiṉ vāyilāka nam uḷḷattil vantu patiyum iv aimporuḷ kaḷip paṟṟiya niṉivukaḷum eṉṉum iraṇṭumē yallāmal vēṟilli eṉpatu iṉitu viḷaṅkāniṟkum. ākavē, kaṭavuḷ ellāp poruḷkaḷiyuṅ kaṭantu niṟpavareṉṟāl, nam aimpoṟikaḷukku viḷaṅkum aimperum poruḷkaḷiyum ap poruḷkaḷip paṟṟi yeḻum niṉivukaḷiyuṅ kaṭantu niṟpavareṉṟē muṭivu ceyyappaṭum. ēṉeṉṟāṟ kaṭavuḷ nāmaṟinta maṇṇil oruvarā? allatu nīril oruvarā? tīyil oruvarā? kāṟṟil oruvarā? allatu iṭiveḷiyā yirukkiṉ ṟaṉarā? eṉṟu kēṭpōmāyiṉ evarum ip poruḷkaḷik kaṭavuḷeṉṟu colla oruppaṭamāṭṭār. allatu ip poruḷkaḷip paṟṟi nam uḷḷatti leḻum ettaṉiyō kōṭi niṉivukaḷil vanta niṉivu kaṭavuḷākum eṉṟu kēṭṭāl, enta niṉiviṉiyuṅ kaṭavuḷeṉṟu colla evarum oruppaṭamāṭṭār. eṉavē, ulakattup poruḷkaḷ etaṉuḷ ḷuṅ kaṭavuḷ aṭaṅkāmal, avikaḷiyellām avar kaṭantu niṟpavar eṉpatē ellā makkaṭkum uṭampāṭāṉa potu uṇmiyākum. nam camayāciriyarāṉa tirunāvukkaracu nāyaṉārum,
virikatir ñāyiṟallar matiyallar vēta
	vitiyallar viṇṇu nilaṉun
  tiritaru vāyuvallar ceṟitīyu mallar
	teḷinīru mallar 
eṉṟu ivvuṇmiyi iṉitu viḷakki aruḷic ceyti rukkiṉṟārkaḷ.
iṉi, ivikaḷiyellām iṟivaṉ kaṭantu niṟpavaṉ eṉṟāl, enta vakiyiṉāl ivikaḷik kaṭantu niṟpavaṉ eṉpatu ārāyntu teḷital vēṇṭum. kaṭattaleṉpati mūṉṟu vakiyākap pākupaṭuttal vēṇṭum. avi: iṭattik kaṭappatuṅ, kālattik kaṭappatum, poruṭ ṭaṉmikaḷik kaṭappatum eṉpaṉavām. avaṟṟuḷ iṭattik kaṭappatāvatu poruḷkaḷellāmirukkum ellā iṭattiyuṅ kaṭantu niṟpatēyākum. nammā laṟiyappaṭṭa entap poruḷkaḷiṉ elliyil allatu enta iṭattiṉ elliyiṟ kaṭavuḷi aṭakkalām eṉṟu evarik kēṭpiṉuṅ kaṭavuḷ entap poruḷiṉ elliyilum aṭaṅkār, enta iṭattiṉ elliyilum aṭaṅkār eṉṟē viṭi kūṟā niṟpar. ātalāṟ kaṭavuḷ iṭattik kaṭantu niṟpavar eṉpatu ellā makkaṭkum oppa muṭintatēyākum.
iṉik, kālattik kaṭantavar eṉpatu mukkālattiyuṅ kaṭantu niṟpavar eṉpatēyām. avar entak kālattil tōṉṟiṉavar, entakkālattil vaḷarkiṉṟavar, entak kālattil muṭivaṭipavar eṉṟu evarik kēṭpiṉum, avar orukālattiṟ ṟōṉṟiyavarumallar, maṟṟorukālattil vaḷarpavarumallar, piṟitorukālattil muṭipavarumallar eṉṟē ellārum viṭi kūṟāniṟpar. atu paṟṟiyē,
ellār piṟappum iṟappumiyaṟ pāvalartam
  collāṟ ṟeḷintēmnañ cōṇēcar-illiṟ
  piṟantakati yuṅkēḷēm pērulakil vāḻntuṇṭu
  iṟantakati yuṅkēṭṭi lēm. 
eṉṟu āṉṟōr tirumoḻiyum eḻuntatu.
iṟivaṉ ellāvaṟṟiṟkum muṟpaṭṭa kālantoṭṭē iruppavaṉ eṉṟu kūṟutaluṅkūṭa avaṉatu iṟimit taṉmikku iḻukkām eṉpatu paṟṟiyē māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ,
muṉṉip paḻamporuṭku muṉṉip paḻamporuḷē
  piṉṉip putumikkum pērttumap peṟṟiyaṉē
eṉṟum,
mūppāṉ iḷiyavaṉ muṉṉavaṉ piṉṉavaṉ
  muppuraṅkaḷ vīppāṉ
eṉṟum aruḷic ceyvārāyiṉar.
iṉip poruḷkaḷiṉ taṉmiyik kaṭantavareṉpatu, avvap poruḷkaḷiṉ iyalpukaḷilun tam miyalpu aṭaṅkāmal avvellāvaṟṟiṉ iyalpukaḷiyum muṟṟuṅ kaṭantu niṟpavar eṉpatēyām. iṉip poruḷkaḷiṉ iyalpu kaḷāvaṉa eviyeṉṟu ārāyvōmāyiṉ, avi vaṇṇam vaṭivu aḷavu cuvi yeṉṉum nālvakiyil aṭaṅkum. vaṇṇamāvatu civappu, nīlam, mañcaḷ, kaṟuppu, ūtā, pacci, veḷḷi eṉṉum eḻuvaki niṟaṅkaḷēyākum. vaṭivāvatu vaṭṭam, nāṟkōṇam, mukkōṇam mutaliyaṉavākum. aḷavāvatu eṇṇalaḷavi, eṭuttalaḷavi, mukattalaḷavi, nīṭṭalaḷaviyeṉa nāṉkām. cuviyāṉatu tittippu, puḷippu, kārppu, kippu, uvarppu, tuvarppu eṉa aṟuvakic cuvi kaḷēyākum. iṅṅaṉam vakuttuk koṇṭa iyalpukaḷuḷ kaṭavuḷ enta niṟattiṉar, enta vaṭiviṉar, enta aḷaviṉar, enta cuviyiṉar eṉṟu evarik kēṭpiṉum, avar iṉṉa niṟattiṉa rāyiruppar, iṉṉa vaṭiviṉarāyiruppar, iṉṉa aḷaviṉarā yiruppar, iṉṉa cuviyiṉarāyiruppar eṉa uṟutippaṭuttic colla māṭṭuvārallar. ākavē kaṭavuḷ poruḷkaḷiṉ elliyiṉiyum iṭattiṉelliyiṉiyuṅ kālattiṉel liyiṉiyuṅ kaṭantu niṟpavarātalōṭu, avvap poruḷ kaḷiṉ iyalpukaḷilēyum aṭaṅkātavarāy avaṟṟi yuṅ kaṭantu niṟṟal ellārkkum otta karuttēyātal iṉitu viḷaṅkukiṉṟataṉṟō? iṅṅaṉamāka ellāvaṟṟiyum ellā vaṟṟiṉiyalpukaḷiyuṅ kaṭavuḷ kaṭantu niṟpavarāy uḷḷavar eṉpatu kaṭavuḷ eṉṉuñ coṟporuḷāl naṉku teḷiyak kiṭakkiṉṟamiyiṉ, iv vuṇmiyip pallāyira āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉṉamē ārāyntuṇarnta nam paḻan tamiḻāciriyarkaḷ, nammā laṟiyappaṭṭa poruḷvakikaḷilum uyirvakikaḷilum av uyirkaḷiṉ tōṟṟa oṭukka vaki kaḷilum oṉṟāka ak kaṭavuḷik kīḻk koṇarntu vittuc collutaṟku oru ciṟitum maṉa miciyātavarkaḷāy avaṉip piṟavāṉ iṟavāṉ eṉṟē yāṇṭum valiyuṟuttic colvā rāyiṉar. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār, piṟappu iṟappukkaḷukku vittākiya viṉiyiṉiṉṟum iṟivaṉ nīṅki niṟpavaṉ eṉpati
viṉiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟiviṉ muṉivaṉ
eṉṟum, piṟappukku mutalvarāṉa tāyun tantiyum illātavaṉ iṟivaṉ eṉpati māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ,
kōyil cuṭukāṭu kolpulittōl nallāṭi
  tāyumili tantiyili tāṉtaṉiyaṉ kāṇēṭi
eṉṟun, tiruñāṉacampantap perumāṉ,
venta cāmpal viriyeṉap pūciyē
  tanti yāroṭu tāyilar tammiyē
  cinti yāeḻu vārviṉi tīrpparāl
  enti yāravar evvaki yārkolō,
eṉṟuñ, camaṇcamaya muṉivarāṉa iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ,
piṟavā yākkip periyōṉ kōyilum
eṉṟum aruḷic ceyvārāyiṉar. piṟkālattu viṇava camayattil uṟittuniṉṟa villiputtūrāḻvāruṅ kūṭa vēṭṭu vaṉvaṭiyil vantu aruccuṉaṉoṭu pōrpurintu avaṉ kivillāl aṭiyuṇṭa iṟivaṉip paṟṟi moḻiyum pōtu,
vētamaṭi yuṇṭaṉa virintapala ākama
	vitaṅkaḷaṭi yuṇṭaṉa ōrim
 pūtamaṭi yuṇṭaṉavi nāḻikimu taṟpukalcey
	poḻutoṭu calippil poruḷiṉ
  pētamaṭi yuṇṭaṉa piṟappili iṟappili
	piṟaṅkal aracaṉṟaṉ makaḷār
  nātamama laṉcamara vēṭavaṭi vaṅkoṭu
	naraṉkiyaṭi yuṇṭa poḻutē.
 eṉṟu iv uṇmiyi oḷiyāmal naṉkeṭuttuk kūṟiṉār.

3. māṇikkavācakar aruḷicceyta kēṭṭārum aṟiyātāṉ 
eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷiṉ uri
ivvāṟellām iv ulakattiluḷḷa ellā makkaṭkum icintatākiya kaṭavuḷiṉ potu ilakkaṇattiyum, aṅṅaṉam evarkkum eṭṭātavarāy ellāmvalla iṟivaṉ iruppiṉum empōṉṟa ciṟṟaṟiviṉārkkuk kāṇavuṅ karutavum paṭāta ak kaṭanta niliyilēyē avaṉ niṟpaṉāyiṉ avaṉatu tiruvaru ḷutaviyiṉṟi ippiṟavit tuṉpatti, ippiṟavi vittukku mūlamāṉa aṟiyāmiyi yāmē nīkkik koḷḷa māṭṭāmiyāl avaṉ tāyiṉum mikka irakkamum aṉpum vittu emmaṉōrkku eḷivantu tōṉṟi aruḷ ceyyum ciṟappilakkaṇattiyum oruṅku viḷakki nam civa camaya mutalāciriyarāṉa māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ aruḷic ceytirukkum oru tiruppāṭṭiṉi īṇṭu ārāyntu pārppōmāka. at tiruppāṭṭu varumāṟu:
kēṭṭāru maṟiyātāṉ kēṭoṉ ṟillāṉ
	kiḷiyilāṉ kēḷātē ellāṅ kēṭṭāṉ
  nāṭṭārkaḷ viḻittirukka ñālat tuḷḷē
	nāyiṉukkut taviciṭṭu nāyi ṉēṟkē
  kāṭṭā taṉavellāṅ kāṭṭip piṉṉuṅ
 	kēḷā taṉavelāṅ kēṭpit teṉṉi
  mīṭṭēyum piṟavāmaṟ kāttāṭ koṇṭāṉ
	emperumāṉ ceytiṭṭa vicci tāṉē.
arumaruntaṉṉa ic ceyyuḷiṉkaṇ nāmeṭuttuk koṇṭa ivvirivurip poruḷiṉ mutaṟkaṇṇatākiya kaṭavuḷ niliyi māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ naṉku virittu viḷakkiyirukkiṉṟā rātaliṉ, icceyyuṭ coṟporuḷi ovvoṉṟāy ārāyntu pārppatu namakkup peritum payaṉpaṭumeṉak karuti avaṟṟi ārāyap pukukiṉṟām.
mutaliṟ kēṭṭāru maṟiyātāṉ eṉṉuñ coṟṟoṭar aṟiviṟ ciṟanta ettakiyiṉari viṉaviyum iṟivaṉ aṟiyappaṭātavaṉ eṉpataṉi viḷakkukiṉṟatu. iṟivaṉiruk kum ūrētu, avaṉ pērētu, avaṉip peṟṟār yār? ap peṟṟōriṉ peyar eṉṉi? eṉṟu nām evari viṉāviṉālum avarellām avaṟṟiṟku viṭi colla māṭṭāmiyiṉ, viṉāviyavar aṅṅaṉa mellām viṉāviyum avaṉi aṟintu koḷḷāta niliyilēyē irukkiṉṟaṉar. iṉṉun tavamuyaṟciyiṟ ṟērnta muṉivarkaḷuṅ kūṭa iṟivaṉ iyalpukaḷik kēṭṭaṟiyap pukuntu tamakkumēl niṟkum muṉivarkaḷik kēṭka, avarkaḷum avaṟṟit tamakkut teriyātu eṉṟu tamakku mēṟpaṭṭa muṉivarkaḷik kēṭka, avarun tamakkut teriyātu eṉṟu tamakku mēṟpaṭṭārik kāṭṭik kiviṭa, iṅṅaṉamē evar evarik kēṭpiṉum avarellām avaṉṟaṉ muḻumutaṟṟaṉmikaḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭa muṭiyāmal varunti niṟṟaliṉ kēṭṭāru maṟiyātāṉ eṉṟu mutaṟkiḷan tōtiṉār.
iṉik, kēṭoṉṟillāṉ eṉṉuñ coṟṟoṭar iṟivaṉ ekkālattum ettakiya aḻivum ettakiya ūṟu pāṭum illātavaṉ eṉpati aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟatu. avaṉ oru kālattil illāmaliruntu piṉṉiyoru kālattut tōṉṟiṉavaṉāy iruppiṉaṉṟō avvāṟu orukālattut tōṉṟiṉavaṉ piṉṉē oru kālattu iṟantu kēṭuṟuvā ṉeṉṟu koḷḷal vēṇṭum. kaṭavuḷukkut tāy tanti yāvār yāvareṉṟu evarik kēṭpiṉum avarellām avaṟkut tāytantiyar ilareṉṟē collutalāl, evarālum avaṉ piṟappik kēṭṭaṟiya muṭiyāmiyiṉ avaṉ piṟavātavaṉ eṉpatu teḷiyappaṭum. avvāṟu teḷiyap paṭavē avaṉukku iṟappum illiyeṉpatu tāṉē pōtarum. evvevvuyir piṟakkiṉṟaṉavō aviyellām iṟakkiṉṟaṉa. piṟavi yeṭutta uyirkaḷukkē iṟappum nikaḻak kāṇkiṉ ṟōmēyallatu piṟavātavikaḷ iṟattali yāṇṭuṅ kaṇṭilēm.
piṟappuṇṭēl iṟappuṇṭām, iṟappuṇṭēl piṟappuṇṭām eṉṉum mūtaṟiñar moḻiyum iv vuṇmi yiṉiyē valiyuṟuttukiṉṟatu. piṟantiṟappaṉavellāñ ciṟṟaṟivuñ ciṟu toḻilumuṭiya uyirkaḷeṉṟum, piṟantiṟa vātavaṉ oruvaṉē iṟivaṉ eṉṟum pakuttuṇarntu koḷvatu mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātatākum. ivvāṟu uyir kaḷukkum iṟivaṉukkum uḷḷa vēṟupāṭṭiṉiyum av vēṟupāṭṭukku ētuvāṉa ilakkaṇaṅkaḷiyuṅ kaṭip piṭiyāka uṇarntu koḷḷavēṇṭuvatu perumpayaṉ peṟa vēṇṭum makkaḷiṉ meyyuṇarccikku mutaṉmiyākum. ivvēṟupāṭṭiṉik, kaṭavuḷukkuriya piṟantiṟavāc ciṟappilak kaṇatti mutaṉmutaṟ kaṇṭaṟintavarkaḷ civacamaya naṉmakkaḷē yāvar. paḻiya yūtarkaḷum, makamatiyarkaḷun tavira maṟṟac camayattavarkaḷellārum piṟantiṟakkum uyirkaḷiyē avvavarpāṟ kāṇappaṭṭa cila āṇmic ceyalkaḷāṟ kaṭavu ḷākap piṟaḻa uṇarntu avi tammiyē kaṭavuḷ niliyil vittu vaṇaṅki varukiṉṟārkaḷ. āṉāl it tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ kaṇ uḷḷa civacamayattavarkaṇ maṭṭum paṇṭikkālan toṭṭup piṟantiṟavāt taṉmiyaṉē muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷāvaṉ eṉat teḷiya uṇarntu iṉṟukāṟum ak koḷkiyiṟ ciṟitum vaḻuvātavarkaḷāy nilipēṟuṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉar. piṟappiṟappillā ik kaṭavuḷilakkaṇatti nam civacamaya āciriyarum piṟarum valiyuṟuttip pāṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷi muṉṉarē nuṅkaṭku eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉēm. iṉṉum nam cantāṉāciriyarākiya aruṇanti civaṉārum,
yātoru teyvaṅ koṇṭīr atteyva māki āṅkē
  mātoru pāka ṉārtām varuvarmaṟ ṟattey vaṅkaḷ
  vētaṉippaṭum piṟakkum iṟakkum mēlviṉiyuñ ceyyum
  ātalāl ivii lātāṉ aṟintuaruḷ ceyva ṉaṉṟē
eṉṟu variyaṟuttuk kūṟiya arumit tiruppāṭṭum nammaṉōr ovvoruvar uḷḷattiluṅ kaṉmēṟcetukkiya eḻuttuppōl nilipeṟṟu niṉṟu niṉivukūraṟ pālatākum.
aṅṅaṉamāyiṉum, muṉṉē yūtarkaḷum makamatiyar kaḷuṅkūṭak kaṭavuḷukkup piṟappiṟappuc collavilli yeṉṟamiyāṟ civa naṉmakkaḷē ak kaṭavuḷilakkaṇatti aṟintārkaḷeṉpatu yāṅṅaṉameṉiṉ, avarkaḷ kaṭavuḷukkup piṟappiṟappuc collāviṭṭāluṅ kaṭavuḷ piṟantiṟavātavaṉ eṉpataṉiyum uyirkaḷē piṟantiṟappaṉa eṉpataṉiyuñ civacamaya āciriyarkaḷip pōlap pirittuk kāṭṭi avarkaḷ āciriyaṉmār evarun tamatu viviliya vētattilātal kurāṉi lātal valiyuṟuttic colli viḷakkiṉārilli. maṟṟut tamiḻāciriyarkaḷ nūlkaḷilō mikap paḻiyakālan toṭṭē kaṭavuḷ viṉiyiṉ nīṅkiya vaṉeṉṟum, avaṉ piṟavā yākkiya ṉeṉṟun, tanti tāy illātavaṉeṉṟum, ellā uyirkaḷum iṟanta cuṭukāṭṭil tāṉ eṉṟum iṟavātu niṉṟē āṭuvāṉeṉṟum palakālum vaṟpuṟuttic collum meyyu rikaḷ aṭuttaṭuttuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ēṉic camayattavar nūlkaḷilō iṅṅaṉam variyaṟutturikkum meyyuri yiṉik kāṇṭaliyalātu. iṟivaṉilakkaṇattit teḷiya uṇartalum iyalātu. ākavē, piṟappu vaḷarppukaḷ oruvāṟṟā ṉuṅ kēṭṭaṟiyappaṭāta mutalvaṉukku evvaki yāṉa kēṭum ekkālattum evviṭattum nikaḻāteṉpatu terittaṟ poruṭ ṭākavē kēṭoṉṟillāṉ eṉṉuñ coṟṟoṭarik kēṭṭāru maṟiyātāṉ eṉṉuñ coṟ ṟoṭarkkup piṉṉē peritum iyipupaṭavittu māṇikka vācakap perumāṉ aruḷic ceyvārāyiṉar.
iṉik, kiḷiyilāṉ eṉpatu cuṟṟattāri illāta vaṉ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum. uṟaviṉarik kuṟikkuñ cuṟṟam kēḷ uṟavu mutalāṉa pala tamiḻc coṟkaḷirukkavum avaṟṟi yellām viṭṭuk kiḷiyeṉṉun tamiḻc collāl aṭikaḷ ataṉi aruḷic ceytirukkum nuṭpam ārāyaṟpālatu. kiḷi yeṉṉuñcol oruvarkkum avarōṭu uṭampiṉ toṭarpu uṭiyārkkum uḷḷa neruṅkiya iciviṉit terivikku māṟupōl maṟṟac coṟkaḷ terivikkavilli. kiḷi yeṉpatu mutaṉ mutal marattiṉ kōṭukaḷukkē peyarāka vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatākum. oru maramum am marattiṉiṉṟu pala kavarkaḷākap piriyuṅ kiḷikaḷum oṉṟiviṭṭoṉṟu vēṟākāmaṟ peritum iyintu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. iṅṅaṉamē oruvaṉukkuk kiḷikaḷ pōl neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyarātaṟ kuriyār; avaṉ tāṉ piṟattaṟku iṭamāṉa tāy tantiyarun, taṉṉōṭuṭaṉ piṟanta tamiyaṉ tampi mutaliyavarun, tammiṉiṉṟu piṟakkum makkaḷum, am makkaḷiṉiṉṟu piṟakkum makkaḷun, taṉṉōṭuṭaṉ piṟantāriṉiṉṟu piṟakkum makkaḷum, am makkaḷukku makkaḷum eṉa avaṉōṭu neruṅkiya uṟavu vāyntārē yāvar. maṟṟac cuṟṟattār uṟaviṉar kēḷir eṉpavarellām avaṉukku attuṇi nerukkam uṭiyā rallar. atupaṟṟiyē tāṉ oruvaṉukku mika neruṅkiya uṭampiṉ toṭarpuṭiyārik kuṟittaṟku māṇikka vācakap perumāṉ kiḷiyeṉṉuñ collic ciṟanta toṉṟāka eṭuttuk koṇṭār.
iṉi, iṟivaṉ tāṉ piṟattaṟku iṭaṉāṉa tāy tantiyarun, taṉṉō ṭuṭaṉpiṟantārun, taṉakku makkaḷā vārum, am makkaḷukku makkaḷāvārun, taṉṉuṭaṉ piṟan tārkku makkaḷāvārum, am makkaḷukkup putalvarāvārum illātava ṉeṉpatu muṉṉamē peṟappaṭṭamiyāl, attakiya neruṅkiya cuṟṟat toṭarpu ciṟitumillātavaṉ eṉpati aṟivittaṟkē kiḷiyilāṉ eṉṟār. kiḷiñarākiya peruntoṭarpu illātavarkaḷukku at toṭarpu paṟṟi varum maṟṟic cuṟṟattārum illāmi tāṉē peṟap paṭumātalāl, iṟivaṉukku vēṟu akaṉṟa cuṟṟattārum illi eṉpatu itu koṇṭu muṭikkappaṭum. neruṅkiya uṭampiṉ toṭarpi liruntē ēṉi ellāc cuṟṟaṅkaḷum ac cuṟṟaṅkaḷip paṟṟi varum palvēṟu allalkaḷum oṉṟilirun toṉṟāyk kiḷikkak kāṇṭalāl, av vellāvaṟṟiṟkum aṭippaṭiyāṉa uṭampiṉ toṭarpiṉi iṟivaṉ oru ciṟitu millātavaṉ eṉpatu terittaṟkē uṭampiṉ toṭarpu kkup peyarāṉa kiḷiyeṉṉuñ colli eṭuttāṇṭu ac collāl evvakit toṭakkumillāta iṟivaṉiyalpic curuṅkac collal āḻamuṭittātal eṉṉum aḻaku peṟak kūṟiya aṭikaḷiṉaṟiviṉ tiṟatti eṉṉeṉpēṉ!
aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, eṇṇiṟanta uyirkaḷ iṟivaṉ eṉṟuḷḷāṉō aṉṟutoṭṭē avaṉiṭattil tāmum tammiṭattil avaṉumāka neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyarāy iruttalāl uyirkaḷi avaṉukkuk kiḷiñarākak kūṟutal poruttamaṉṟō eṉiṟ, kiḷiñareṉpār oruvaruk koruvar utaviyun tuṇiyumāy irukkun taṉmiyarāvar. itu kiḷiyeṉṉuñ colliṉ mutaṟporuḷiṉālēyē iṉitu viḷaṅkā niṟkum. eṅṅaṉameṉiṉ, marattāṟ kiḷiyuṅ kiḷiyāl maramum uyirvāḻkiṉṟaṉa. aṭimaratti veṭṭi ṉāṟ kiḷikaḷ paṭṭuppōm. kiḷikaḷiyellām veṭṭi ṉāl maramum paṭṭuppōm. itu perumpālum ellā maraṅ kaḷiṭattuṅ kāṇappaṭum uṇmi nikaḻcciyām. itupōlavē tāytantiyar tam makkaḷ iḷiñarāyirukkum poḻutu avarkku utaviyun tuṇiyumāy niṉṟu avarip pātukāttu uyirvāḻac ceykiṉṟaṉar. makkaḷ vaḷarntu periyarām poḻutu tam tāytantiyar āṇṭiṉ mutirntu valivukuṉṟap peṟutalāl makkaḷ avarkku utaviyun tuṇiyumāy niṉṟu avari uyirvāḻac ceykiṉṟaṉar. iṅṅaṉamē uṭaṉ piṟantārum oruvarukkoruvar utaviyun tuṇiyumāy 
niṉṟu uyirvāḻtali nērē kāṇkiṉṟōm. ākavē, kiḷiñarāvā rellārum oruvarukkoruvar utaviyun tuṇiyumāy niṉṟu oḻukiṉāl maṭṭum avar kiḷiñareṉṟu peyar peṟutaṟ kuriyāreṉpatu peṟappaṭum. inta muṟiyil vittuk kaṭavuḷuṅ kaṭavuḷoṭu toṉṟu toṭṭu irukkum uyir kaḷum oruvarukkoruvar utaviyuṅ tuṇiyumāyiruntu uyir vāḻun taṉmiyarāyiṉ, avar tammiyuṅ kiḷiñareṉṟu moḻital poruntum. kaṭavuḷ uyirkaḷukku uṟṟa tuṇiyum utaviyumāy niṟṟal pōla uyirkaḷuṅ kaṭavuḷukku uṟṟa tuṇiyum utaviyumāy niṉṟu avari uyir vāḻac ceyku varāyiṉ, avari avarkkuk kiḷiñareṉṟu kūṟutal poruttamām. maṟṟu uyirkaḷō paṇṭu toṭṭē aṟiyāmi yeṉṉum pēriruḷāl viḻuṅkap paṭṭut tammiyum uṇarāmal tam uyirkkuyirāy niṉṟu tamakkup pērutavi ceytuvarum mutalvaṉiyum uṇarāmal valiyaṟṟa niliyiṉarāy irutta lālum, iṟi vaṉuṭiya utaviyip peṟṟu aṟivu viḷaṅkit tammiyun talivaṉiyum uṇarumnili vanta ñāṉṟun tammuṭiya aṟivuñ ceyalum iṟivaṉṟaṉ pēraṟivu pērāṟṟalkaḷiṉ muṉ valiviḻantaṉa vāyē iruppatallatu av viṟivaṟkut tam aṟivālun tam ceyalāluñ ceyyattakka utavi ciṟitu millāmiyālum, ellāc celvamuñ ceyalum uṭiya iṟivaṉukkumuṉ av uyirkaḷ, ceyaliḻanta vaṟiya iravalarē yāvarallatu avaṉukkuk kiḷiñarātal oruvāṟṟā ṉuñ cellātu. iṅṅaṉam eṇṇiṟanta av uyirt toki kaḷi yellām ellāmvalla iṟivaṉukkuk kiḷiñarākac collutal oruvāṟṟāṉum iciyāmiyāl, iv uṇmiyi naṉkuṇarnta māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ kiḷiyilāṉ eṉṟu aruḷicceytāreṉpatu.
iṉik, kēḷātē ellāṅ kēṭṭāṉ eṉṉuñ coṟṟoṭar piṟar colvaṉavaṟṟic ceviyeṉṉuṅ karuviyāṟ kēṭṭu aṟiyum nammaṉōr pōlātu, ak karuviyillāmalē ulakiṉ kaṇṇuḷḷa ellā makkaḷuñ colluñ coṟkaḷiyum niṉikkum niṉivukaḷiyum aṟiya vallāṉ eṉavum, vāyeṉṉuṅ karuvi koṇṭu piṟarpāl aṟiya vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟik kēṭṭaṟiyum nammaṉōr pōlātu ak karuviyiṉ utaviyillāmalē piṟar uḷḷattu nikaḻum nikaḻccikaḷiyellān taṉ niṉiviṉālēyē uṇara vallāṉ eṉavum iruporuḷ taruvatām. makkaṭpiṟavi yiṉarākiya nām ceviyiṉutaviyiṉṟip piṟar colvaṉavaṟṟik kēṭṭuṇarum āṟṟalillātavarkaḷāyirukkiṉṟōm. vāyiṉu taviyiṉṟip piṟari viṉāvit teriya vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟit terintu koḷḷa iyalātavarkaḷāyirukkiṉṟōm. ceviyum vāyu millāta ceviṭarum ūmarum ak karuvikaḷ illāmiyāṟ paṭun tuṉpattik kaṇṭu evvaḷavu irakkamuṟu kiṉṟōm. tammiṉum aṟiviṟ ciṟantavarkaḷilliyeṉavun, tammiṉum valimiyiṟ ciṟantavarkaḷilliyeṉavum iṟumāntu niṉṟōr kuruṭar ūmar mutalāṉa uṟuppaṟikaḷik kaṇṭu tāmum avarkaḷip pōṟ paḻutupaṭṭiruntāl tammuṭiya niliyum evvaḷavu tuṉpuṟat takkatā yirukkumeṉṟu niṉintu ataṉāl tamatu ciṟumiyum ellāmvalla iṟivaṉṟaṉ perumiyum uṇarntu neñcam nekkurukutalik kāṇkiṉṟōmallamō? iṅṅaṉamē ivvariya uṭampil aminta maṟṟip poṟikaḷum maṟṟi uṟuppukkaḷum paḻutupaṭṭup pōkumāṉāl, avvuṭam piṉkaṇ niṟku muyir ataṉāl aṟivu viḷaṅkap peṟātu ētoru ceyalumiṉṟi veṟuṅkallip pōṟ kiṭakkumaṉṟō? iṟivaṉ aruḷkaṉintu aḷittaruḷiya ivvuṟuppukaḷiṉ utaviyi muḻutum peṟṟu uyir vāḻuñ cilar avvutaviyit tiṉittuṇiyum niṉintupārāmal yāmē kaṭavuḷeṉṟu cerukkip pēcā niṟpar. avaruṭiya cerukkellām avartam uṟuppukaḷ cīrkulintu pōṉāl nilikkumō coṉ miṉkaḷ aṉparkaḷē! 
ivvāṟāka uṭampiṉutaviyum uṭampilaminta uṟuppu kaḷiṉutaviyum iṉṟi aṟivuñ ceyaluṅ kikūṭap peṟāta nammaṉōr nilimikkum, ivvuṟuppukaḷiṉ utavi ciṟitum vēṇṭāmalē eṇṇiṟanta ulakaṅkaḷiyum avvulakaṅkaḷi luḷḷa eṇṇiṟanta kōṭi uyirkaḷiyuṅ kāttum paṭittuṅ karantum viḷiyāṭi varum ellām valla aiyaṉuṭiya nili mikkum oppumi eṅkē! toṉṟu toṭṭē pēriruḷākiya aṟiyāmiyiṟ kiṭantu aṟivoḷi maḻuṅkip piṉṉar iṟivaṉ tanta uṭampālum avvuṭampiṉ uṟuppukaḷāluñ ciṟituciṟitāka aṟivu viḷaṅkap peṟṟu varum nammaṉōrkkē ūṉuṭampiṉ karuvikaḷ vēṇṭappaṭuvataṉṟi, ik karuvikaḷip paṭittuk koṭukkum ellāmvalla iṟivaṉukku ik karuvikaḷum ivaṟṟiṉutavikaḷuñ ciṟitum vēṇṭappaṭā; itu paṟṟiyē,
ceviyiṉāṟ kēḷātatāyc ceviyiṉik kēṭpippatāy uḷḷatetuvō atuvē piramam eṉṟum, vāyiṉāṟ pēcātatāy vāyiṉip pēcuvippatāy uḷḷa tetuvō atuvē piramam eṉṟum aṟi; ataṉ pakkattēyiruntu ataṉāl iyakkappaṭukiṉṟa nī piramamākāy.
eṉak kēnōpaniṭatam vaṟpuṟuttuk kūṟuvatāyiṟṟu.. ivvuṇmiyiṉiyē māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ, kēḷātē ellāṅ kēṭṭāṉ eṉṉuñ coṟṟoṭarāl teḷiyak kūṟiṉār.
eṉṟu itukāṟuṅ kēṭṭāru maṟiyātāṉ, kēṭoṉṟil lāṉ, kiḷiyilāṉ, kēḷātē yellāṅ kēṭṭāṉ eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṟ peṟappaṭṭa kaṭavuḷ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ avvaḷa vum in nilavulakattuḷḷa ellā makkaḷālum ellāc camayattārālum ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭuvaṉavēyākum. ākavē ellārkkum uṭaṉpāṭāṉa ivi kaṭavuḷukkuriya potu ilakkaṇaṅka ḷeṉappaṭum. ippotu ilakkaṇaṅkaḷāl, ip potu vilakkaṇaṅkaḷi uṭiyavarāka nammāl ārāyntaṟiyap paṭṭa kaṭavuḷnili, ciṟṟaṟiviṉarākiya namatu collukkum niṉivukkum eṭṭātatāyirukkiṉṟatu. epporuḷuṅ kaṭanta mutalvaṉi, evarāluṅ kēṭṭaṟiyappaṭāta talivaṉi, evvaki aḻivukkum akappaṭāta āṇṭavaṉi, evvakip paṭṭa kiḷiñarumillāta taṉṉan taṉiyaṉi, yāmaṟiyātē yāmkēḷātē irukkavum emakku vēṇṭuvaṉavellān tantu taliyaḷittuvarun tōṉṟāt tuṇiyi ēḻiyēm eṅṅaṉaṅ kāṇpēm? mtdJ mU£nguÄœj¤ij v§‡d« gUFnt«?emmi iṭiyaṟātu paṟṟit toṭarntuvarum piṟavit tuṉpatti eṅṅaṉam nīkkuvēm? eṉṟu eṇṇi eṇṇi neñcaṅ kuḻiyāniṉṟa nēraṅkaḷil avaṉatu eṭṭā nilimi yil em maṉam paṟṟuvatiṉṟāy avaṉ tiruvuruviṉik kāṇa vum avaṉ tiruvaṭikaḷip paṟṟik koṇṭu tēmpavum viḻintu eṉpillāp puḻuviṉuṅ kaṭippaṭṭa emmiyum kaṭikkaṇippaṉō eṉṟu ematuḷḷam ēṅkukiṉṟataṉṟō? attakiya aṭaṅkā ēkkattiṉpāṟpaṭṭa em ciṟṟuyirkkuk kaṭavuḷiṉ eṭṭā nilimiyic collic collik kāṭṭuvatāl em ēkkaṅ tīrumō? tīrātaṉṟē! avaṉ, taṉṉil evvaḷavu periya ṉāyiṉum evvaḷavu eṭṭā nilimi yaṉāyiṉum em pōṉṟa ciṟṟuyirkaḷukku iraṅki eḷivantu tōṉṟi aruḷ ceyyiṉaṉṟō ematu ēkkan tīrum, appōtaṉṟō ema tuḷḷam ivvulakatti maṟantu, iṅkuḷḷa toṭarpukaḷi maṟantu mēleḻumpi avaṉ tiruvaṭikaḷiṟ paṭiyum; appōtaṉ ṟō kaṭavuḷiṉ uṇmi nili emakku viḷaṅkum. atukāṟuñ carukkariyik kaṇṭaṟiyātavaṉ, ataṉ cuviyic cuvittaṟiyātavaṉ ataṉ iṉimiyiṉi viyantu viyantu pēcic calippaṭivatallatu ataṉip peṟṟuc cuvittu makiḻumvaki avaṟkuk kikūṭātavāṟu pōl, yāmum kaṭavuḷiṉ eṭṭāta nilikaḷi, avaṉṟaṉ pēriṉpap peruñcuviyip pēcippēci eṇṇi eṇṇi vāṭuvatallatu avaṉip peṟṟu avaṉatu pēriṉpavayamāy niṟkum perumpēṟu emakkuk kikūṭumō?
kaṭavuḷ tāmē tōṉṟiyava reṉpatum, avaratu iruppu iṉṟiyamiyāta teṉpatum, māyiyik kaṭantavareṉpatum, avar eḷiyar eṉpatum, ciṟṟuyirkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum varicikkum uḷvēṟṟumikkum avar mēmpaṭṭava reṉpatum, pakukkappaṭātava reṉpatum, uyiruḷ poruḷ kaḷilum iyakkaṅkaḷilum iṉṟiyamiyāt taṉmikaḷilum aviyallāta taṉmikaḷilum maṟintuḷḷa ceyalkaḷilum veḷippaṭṭa ceyalkaḷilum uḷḷa vēṟṟumikaḷ avar uṭiyarallar eṉpatum, avar ōriṉattiṟ cērkkappaṭutalil lātava reṉpatum, meymmi vāynta aḷavilāt  taṉmiya reṉpatun, tām kāṭṭum nalliyalpukaḷukku vēṟāṉa tiruvuruvam vāyntavareṉpatun, tīmiyiṉ iruppukku iṭantaruvarē yaṉṟi at tīmiyiṉi uṇṭu paṇṇupavar allareṉpatun, tāmē ellā muṭiyareṉpatun, tampā laṉpiṉar eṉpatun, tampāl taṉitta pēriṉpatti uṭiya reṉpatum ākiya avaruṭiya iyalpukaḷ ematu vāḻkki yōṭu ev vakiyāṉa toṭarpu koṇṭu niṟkiṉṟaṉa? avaruṭiya ivviyalpukaḷ taṉittaṉiyē tamakku otta vakiyāka ematu oḻukalāṟṟiṉit tiruttik koḷvataṟku iṭañ ceyyāvāyiṉ, avi uṇmiyē yāyiṉum alla tavi poyyēyāyiṉum avi oruvaṉuṭiya camaya vāḻk kikku entavakiyil vēṟupaṭṭa payaṉit tarakkūṭum?
eṉṉaḷavil mēṟkuṟikkappaṭṭa kaṭavuḷ iyalpukaḷip paṟṟiya niṉivukaḷukku māṟāṉavikaḷic colvatil yāṉ veṟuppuṭiyēṉāyiṉum, ivikaḷ kuṟṟamillātaṉavākavē eṭukkappaṭumāyiṉum namatu camaya vāḻkkikku ivi tiṉit tuṇiyāvatu payaṉpaṭumeṉṟu karutakkūṭavilli eṉpatit tiṟantu colkiṉṟēṉ. ākavē, ivi oṉṟum uṇmi yāvaṉa alla. kaṭavuḷ eḷimiyuṭiyarāyiruntāl anta iyalpukkup porutta māmpaṭi yāṉ evvakiyāṉa oḻuka lāṟṟil oḻukakkūṭum, ataṉi coṉmiṉkaḷ! avar taṉip paṭṭa niṟivāṉa iṉpamuṭiyarāṉāl, ataṉi uṇarvata ṉāl yāṉ eṉatu naṭakkiyi evvāṟu vakuttuk koḷḷak kūṭumō ataṉic coṉmiṉkaḷ! attakiya iyalpukaḷip paṟṟiya coṟṟokutikaḷ kaṭavuḷip paṟṟiya uṇmiyāṉa uṇarcciyi emakkut tarukiṉṟaṉavā? kaṭavuḷiruppiṉi nampiya kōṭpāṭukaḷ toṭarpāka nilavu māyiṉum, uṇmic camayamāṉatu in nilavulakattil illāmaṟ paṟantōṭi viṭum. āṉāṟ, payaṉpaṭattakka camaya vāḻkki yiliruntu nōkku vēṉāṉāl, mēṟkuṟitta veṟṟiyalpukaḷ kalvip pulami mikkōr kaṭṭiviṭṭa ciṟitum payaṉpaṭāta kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷē yallāmal avaṟṟi vēṟeṉ ṉeṉṟu viḷampuvēṉ!
iṉi, avi aṅṅaṉamirukka namatu oḻukkamuṟikku otta kaṭavuḷiṉ iyalpukaḷic ciṟitu ārāyvām. ivviyalpu kaḷō accattiyum nampikkiyiṉiyum nilipeṟac ceytu tūya tuṟavu vāḻkkikku aṭippaṭi kōlukiṉṟaṉa. yāṅṅaṉa meṉiṟ, kaṭavuḷ tūyataṉmi yuṭiyareṉṟu koṇṭāl, avar uyirkaḷiṉ naṉmiyiṉiyaṉṟi vēṟoṉṟaṉiyuṅ karutār. avar ellām vallareṉṟu koṇṭāl avar an naṉmiyiṉi uyirkaḷukku muṭittuttara vallunarāvar. avar ellām uṇarpavar eṉṟu koṇṭāl avar iruḷilum nammik kāṇa vallunarāvar. avar naṭuniliyāḷareṉṟu koṇṭāl nammuṭiya tavaṟukaḷik kaṇṭu avar oṟukka vallunarāvar. avar aṉpuṭiyareṉak koṇṭāl nam iḻukkaṅkaḷi avar maṉṉikkavum vallunarāvar. avar māṟāta iyaṟkiyareṉṟu koṇṭāl avaratu utaviyil nām nampiyirukkak kūṭum. ittakiya iyalpukaḷē namatu vāḻkkiyōṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyaṉavāka irutta lāl, ivikaḷip paṟṟi nām terintu koḷvatu mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātatākum. kaṭavuḷ ulakattip paṭittataṉ nōkkam avar tamatu pēraruṭ ciṟappiṉi uyirkaḷukku vaḻaṅka vēṇṭu meṉpatē yākiyāl ivviyalpum namatu vāḻkki yōṭu tiṭṭamāṉa toṭarpuṭiyatāka viḷaṅkāniṟkiṉṟatu1 eṉavum, kaṭanta niliyiṉik kūṟum māyāvāta ārāycci yurikaḷ camaya uṇmiyiṉi ulakamellān taḻuvumpaṭi ceyya māṭṭātavaṉavāy vaḻuvukiṉṟaṉa2 eṉavum amerik kāvil meyyuṇarvi ṉarāy viḷaṅkiya u.villiyam jēm eṉṉuñ cāṉṟōr āḻntu ārāyntu uritta aṟivurikaḷ, kaṭavuḷiṉ potuviyal pākiya kaṭanta nilimiyiṉi vaṟpuṟuttu urikkuṅ kōṭpāṭukaḷ ciṟṟuyirkaḷākiya namakku evvāṟṟāṉum payaṉpaṭā eṉpatiyum, avar ciṟṟuyirka ḷellāvaṟṟiṟkum vēṇṭuvatākiya aḻiyāp perum pēṟṟi nalkutaṟkum ataṉi nalkutaṟporuṭṭu eḷiyarāy vantu avi tammi āṭ koḷḷutaṟkum avarkku iyaṟkiyāyuḷḷa aṉpum aruḷum irakkamumākiya arum pēriyalpukaḷē emakku evvāṟṟāṉum payaṉpaṭumeṉpa tiyum iṉitu viḷakkukiṉṟaṉa.
kaṭanta nilimiyaṉāy niṟkum iṟivaṉic ciṟṟuyir kaḷākiya emmuṭaṉ neruṅkat toṭarpu paṭuttuvaṉa, aṉpum aruḷum irakkamumākiya ivviyalpukaḷēyām. ciṟṟuyir kaḷākiya ematu iv vulaka vāḻkkiyi ṉuḷḷum oruvarōṭu oruvari maṉamakiḻa icivittu uyirvāḻac ceyvatum aṉpum aruḷum irakkamum allavō? aṉpillātavarkaḷ, piṟa uyirkaḷiṉ tuṉpattik kaṇṭu iraṅkātavarkaḷ oruvarō ṭoruvar kūṭi uyirvāḻtalum utavi vēṇṭum uyirkaḷukku utavipuritalum ulakattil nikaḻak kaṇṭatuṇṭō coṉ miṉkaḷ! itu paṟṟiyaṉṟō āciriyar teyvap pulamit tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉār,
aṉpilār ellān tamakkuriyar aṉpuṭiyār
  eṉpu muriyar piṟarkku.
eṉṟu aruḷic ceyvā rāyiṉar. tāṉīṉṟa kuḻanti nōy vāyppaṭṭu varuntukiyil ūṇum uṟakkamumiṉṟi ataṉ pakkattē iruntu tāyāṉavaḷ kavali koṇṭu varuntumāṟu ceyvatu etu? pōrmēṟ ceṉṟa taṉ kaṇavaṉ iṟantāṉ eṉak kēṭṭu ēṅki uyirtuṟanta kātaliyi aṅṅaṉam uyir tuṟakku māṟu ceytatu etu? tāṉ vaḻipaṭukaṭavuḷiṉ tiruvuruvak kaṇṇiṉiṉṟu cennīr vaṭiya nōkkiya āṟṟātu tam kaṇṇi iṭantu ak kaṭavuḷ vaṭivattiṉ kaṇṇil appiya kaṇṇap  pari aṅṅaṉam appumāṟu ceytatu etu? īṉṟu aṇiya paṉṟik kuṭṭikaḷ tam tāyiṉi iḻantu nilikkaḷaṉ kāṇātu tattaḷittalik kaṇṭu tāṅkāmal tāyppaṉṟi vaṭiveṭuttu avaṟṟiṉ tuṉpattip pōkkiya iṟivaṉi aṅṅaṉam ceyyu māṟu ēviyatu ētu? vēṭaṉāl turattap paṭṭut taṉṉakam pukunta puṟāviṉ allalkaṇṭu, appuṟāviṉ eṭikku īṭākat taṉṉu ṭampiṉ catiyiṉi aṟuttuk koṭutta cōḻa maṉṉaṉi aṅṅaṉañ ceyyumāṟu tūṇṭiyatu etu? eṉṟu naṉku ārāyntu uṇariṉ aṉpum aruḷum irakkamumē avvā ṟellām ceyvavāyiṉa eṉpatu pulappaṭukiṉṟataṉṟō?
ākavē ciṟṟuyirkaḷukkuc, ciṟṟuyirkaḷiṉ aṟivukku, niṉivukku eṭṭāta niliyil niṟkuṅ kaṭavuḷi avvuyir kaḷiṉ aṟivukkum niṉivukkuṅ kāṭcikkuṅkūṭa eṭṭiya niliyilē eḷitākak  koṇṭu vantu kāṭṭavallatu kaṭavuḷi ṭattuḷḷa aruṭṭaṉmiyē eṉpatu naṉku viḷaṅkaṟ pālatām. kaṭavuḷ maṟṟi ellā uyarnta taṉmikaḷum uṭiyarā yiruppiṉum avvaruṭṭaṉmi oṉṟu maṭṭum  ilarāyiṉ ivvulakattiyum ivvulakattuṟṟa uyirkaḷiyum nām kāṇṭaliyalātu. piṟarpaṭun tuṉpattik kaṇṭu irakkappaṭāta oruvar avarkaṭku ētōr utaviyuñ ceyyak kaṇṭatuṇ ṭō? irakkamum īra neñcamum illātavarkaḷ piṟarōṭu aṉpāyp pēciṉum eṅkē avarkku utavi ceyya nērntu viṭumō eṉṟu neñcam naṭunaṭuṅkip piṟaroṭu pēcātum avarōṭu aḷavaḷāvātun taṉittuṟavu niliyi aṭi talik kaṇṭāmaṉṟō? ittakiya tuṟavikaḷuḷḷē kaṭavuḷum oruvarāy viṭuvarāyiṉ, ematu uyirvāḻkki ivvulakattil naṭipeṟumeṉṟu kaṉavilum niṉital kūṭumō? ātaliṉā laṉṟō, nam civacamayat teyvamākiya civapirāṉ taṉṉi ittakiya tuṟavuniliyiṟ cērttuk koḷḷātu kaṅkiyum umiyumākiya irumaṉiviyariyum, piḷḷiyār muruka ṉeṉṉum, iraṇṭu makkaḷiyum uṭiyaṉāyt taṉakku aṉpē vaṭiveṉpati uṇarttalā yiṉāṉ. ivvarum pēruṇmi maṟṟōriṭattum nam māṇikka vācakap perumā ṉāl,
teṉpāl ukantāṭum tillicciṟ ṟampalavaṉ
  peṇpāl ukantāṉ perumpittaṉ kāṇēṭī
  peṇpāl ukantilaṉēṟ pētāy irunilattōr
  viṇpāli yōkeyti viṭuvarkāṇ cāḻalō.
eṉṟu aṟivuṟuttappaṭṭatu.
aṉparkaḷē! iṅkē yām kūṟiyatu koṇṭu ellām valla mutalvaṉukku maṉivimārum makkaḷum uḷarpōlum eṉa niṉintu namatu muḻumutaṟ ṟeyvatti iḻivāka niṉintu viṭēṉmiṉ! maṉivi makkaḷeṉṟa toṭarpellām aṉpiṉip pulappaṭuttum aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷēyām. aṉpum iṉpamumākiya taṉṉiyalpukaḷ taṉṉōṭu toṭarpuṭiyāri ṭattup pulaṉāvaṉavaṉṟi vēṟu tāmākavē pulaṉāvaṉavalla. iṟivaṉuṭiya aṉpiyum iṉpattiyum peṟutaṟku urimi uṭiyarāy avaṉi neruṅki niṟkum nāmellām avaṉukku maṉiviyarum makkaḷumēyāvam. maṉivi makkaḷeṉṟa toṭar pukaḷiṉ uṇmiyi āḻntu ārāyumiṭattu avi aṉpeṉ ṉum vitiyil muḷitteḻuntu ōṅkip paṭarnta tēmāviṉ tēṉoḻuku kaṉikaḷēyām. iṉṉum itaṉi nuṇuki nōkku miṭattu ulakameṅkaṇum pulaṉākātu maṟintu niṟkum oḷi yiṉiyuṅ kata katappiṉiyum pulaṉākak kāṭṭun tiṅkaḷum ñāyiṟumē at toṭarpukaḷām eṉṟu karututalum iḻuk kātu. inta muṟiyilētāṉ kaṭavuḷukkuñ ciṟṟuyir kaḷākiya emakkum uḷḷa toṭarpu karutaṟpāṟ ṟeṉpataṟkē, civa perumāṉukku maṉiviyarum makkaḷum uḷarāka vittup puṉinturi vakiyāl āṉṟōr kūṟuvārāyiṉar. itu koṇṭu kaṭavuḷ tām eṭṭā niliyiṉarāyiṉun, tām an niliyilēyē irukkum viruppu ilarāy, emakkun tamatu pēriṉpatti vaḻaṅkutaṟ poruṭṭu eḻunta pēr aruṭṭaṉ miyāl emakku eḷiyarāy emakku aṇukkarāy yām vēṇṭiyavāṟellām vantu utavi ceyyuñ ciṟappiyalpu vāyntavareṉpatē aṟital vēṇṭum. itu veṟuñ collaḷavil nillātu uṇmiyāka nikaḻvatātali, māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ tamakku iṟavaṉ kaṭpulaṉāka eḻuntaruḷi vantu aruḷ ceyta uṇmi aruḷ nikaḻcciyi ōreṭuttuk kāṭṭāyk kūṟuvāṉ pukuntu,
nāṭṭārkaḷ viḻittirukka ñālat tuḷḷē
	nāyiṉukkut taviciṭṭu nāyi ṉēṟkē
  kāṭṭā taṉavellāṅ kāṭṭi.
eṉṟu aruḷic ceyvārāyiṉār.
itaṉkaṇ nāṭṭārkaḷ viḻittirukka eṉṟatu ulakattiluḷḷa makkaḷellārum iṟivaṉik kāṇṭaṟkum avaṉṟaṉ aruḷ utaviyip peṟutaṟkum vēṭki perituṭiyarāy iṟivaṉ tamakkumuṟ pulappaṭum nēratti etirpārttu atilē kaṇṇuṅ karuttumuṭiyarāy muṉintu niṟṟali viḷakkā niṟkum. makkaḷuḷ ettuṇi iḻintōruṅ kaṭavuḷeṉṉuñ collik kēṭṭa aḷavāṉē avaṉik kāṇṭalil vēṭyuṅ karuttum uṭiyarā yirukkiṉṟaṉar. avvāṟavar vēṭki mikuntuḷḷārāyiṉum, av vēṭkiyiṉpaṭi kaṭavuḷik kāṇum muyaṟciyilēyē aḻunti niṟkamāṭṭāmal ulaka iṉpaṅkaḷil iḻuppuṇṭu pōtaliṉ, avar koṇṭa vēṭki kikūṭātu oḻikiṉṟatu. ivaraṅṅaṉa moḻiya, ip potu makkaḷiṉ vēṟāṉa cilar peruvēṭkiyum perumuyaṟciyum uṭiyarāyt tavamuyaṟciyiṟ pukuntu kaṭavuḷik  kāṇṭalilēyē karuttu oruṅki niṟpar. aṅṅaṉam niṟpārkkuṅ kūṭa iṟivaṉ kaṭpulaṉāyt tōṉṟi aruḷ ceyyātu, tamakku avaṉ nērē tōṉṟi aruḷ ceyta aruṭperun tiṟatti māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ ñālattuḷḷē nāyiṉukkut taviciṭṭu nāyiṉēṟkē, kāṭṭātaṉa vellāṅ kāṭṭi eṉṟu aruḷic ceyvārāyiṉar. iṅṅaṉamē piṟitōriṭattum,
marakatak kuvāal māmaṇip piṟakkam
 miṉṉoḷi koṇṭa poṉṉoḷi tikaḻat
 ticimukaṉ ceṉṟu tēṭiṉark koḷittum
 muṟiyuḷi oṟṟi muyaṉṟavark koḷittum
 oṟṟumi koṇṭu nōkkum uḷḷattu
 uṟṟavar varunta uṟippavark koḷittum
 maṟittiṟa nōkki varuntiṉark koḷittum
ittan tirattiṟ kāṇṭumeṉ ṟiruntōrkku
attan tirattiṉ avvayiṉ oḷittum
muṉivaṟa nōkki naṉivarak kuvi
āṇeṉat tēmāṉṟi aliyeṉap peyarntu
vāṇutaṟ peṇṇeṉa oḷittum cēṇvayiṉ
aimpulaṉ celaviṭuttu aruvari toṟumpōyt
tuṟṟavi tuṟanta veṟṟuyi rākki
aruntavar kāṭciyuḷ tirunta oḷittum
oṉṟuṇ ṭilli yeṉṟaṟivu oḷittum
paṇṭē payiltoṟum iṉṟē payiltoṟum
oḷikkuñ cōraṉik kaṇṭaṉam,
ārmiṉ ārmiṉ nāṇmalarp piṇiyaliṟ
ṟāṭaḷi iṭumiṉ
cuṟṟumiṉ cūḻmiṉ toṭarmiṉ viṭēṉmiṉ
paṟṟumiṉ eṉṟavar paṟṟumuṟ ṟoḷittum
taṉṉē rillōṉ tāṉēyāṉa taṉmi
eṉṉē raṉiyōr kēṭkavan tiyampi
aṟikūvi yāṭkoṇ ṭaruḷi
maṟiyōr kōlaṅ kāṭṭi yaruḷalum   
(tiruvaṇṭappakuti 124 - 149)
eṉṟu aṭikaḷ tiruvāy malarntami kāṇka.
aṟṟēl, tavamuyaṟciyiṟ pukunta muṉivararālum aṟiyappaṭāta mutalvaṉi at tavamuyaṟciyiṉṟi araca vāḻkkiyilirunta māṇikkavācakar avaṉatu aruḷi nērē peṟṟaṉareṉṟalum, tammi nōkkit tavampurinta muṉivararkkum aruḷ vaḻaṅkātu am muyaṟcikkup puṟampāy niṉṟa aṭikaḷukku avvaruḷi vaḻaṅkiṉāṉeṉṟalum iṟivaṉatu aruṭ ṭaṉmikku iḻukkākātō eṉiṉ kūṟutum :
tava muyaṟciyic ceyvārkaḷellān tāñ ceyyum  am muyaṟciyiṉālēyē tāṅkaḷ eṇṇiyavikaḷi eṇṇiyapaṭiyē peṟutal kūṭumeṉṟum, piṟari vāḻvittalun, tāḻvittalun tam collaḷaviṉālēyē nikaḻutal kūṭumeṉṟum, vēṟu em muyaṟcikkuṅ kiṭṭāta kaṭavuḷit tamatu tavamuyaṟciyiṉālēyē eḷitiṟ kiṭṭac ceytal kūṭumeṉṟum muṉippuṭaṉ niṉintu cerukkuṭi  yarāy niṟṟalāl, av varuntavattōr kāṭcikkuṅ kaṭavuḷ pulappaṭātavaṉāyt taṉṉi oḷippaṉ. teyvap pulamit tiruvaḷḷuvanāyaṉārum yāṉ eṉatu eṉṉuñ cerukkuṭiyavarkaḷ ceyyun tavamuyaṟci kaḷellān tēvarkaḷuṟiyun tuṟakkavulakaṅkaḷiṟ celuttu meṉṟum, avviruvakic cerukkum aṟṟu muḻutum aṉpuruvāy niṉṟavarkaḷē vāṉōrkkum mēlāṉa iṟivaṉṟaṉ pēriṉpa vulakappēṟṟiṉi yeytuvareṉṟum naṉku valiyuṟutti
yāṉeṉa teṉṉuñ cerukkaṟuppāṉ vāṉōrkku
  uyarnta ulakam pukum
eṉṟu aruḷic ceytaṉar. namatu ulakiya loḻukkat tilun tammiyun tamataṟiviyun tamatu muyaṟciyiyumē periyaṉavākak karuti iṟumāntu niṟpavarkaḷ piṟarpāl aṉpuṭiyarāy oḻukutalik kāṇkilamaṉṟō? aṉpum cerukkum oḷiyum iruḷumpōlat tammil māṟupaṭṭa iyaṟkiyiṉavākum. aṉpu nikaḻumuḷḷattil iṟumāppu uṇṭākamāṭṭātu; iṟumāppu mīteḻuntu niṟku muḷḷatti lō aṉpu ōreṭṭuṇiyun talikkāṭṭa māṭṭātu. ātalāl tavamuyaṟciyāṉatu mikavum pārāṭṭaṟpāla toṉṟē yāyiṉum, aḵtu ēṉi ulakiyal muyaṟcika ḷellāvaṟṟiyum viṭa mikac ciṟantatē yāyiṉum atu nikaḻuṅkāl yāṉ eṉateṉ ṉuñ cerukkum ataṉōṭu uṭaṉ nikaḻutaliṉ ammuyaṟci yuṭiya muṉivarkkum iṟivaṉ pulappaṭṭut tōṉṟātu oḷippa ṉeṉṟār. ivvuṇmi āciriyar tirumūla nāyaṉārālum
eṉpē viṟakā iṟicci yaṟuttiṭṭup
 poṉpōṟ kaṉaliṟ poriya vaṟuppiṉum
  aṉpō ṭuruki akaṅkuḻi vārkkaṉṟi
  eṉpoṉ maṇiyiṉi yeytavoṇ ṇātē
eṉṟu naṉkeṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭṭatu. piṟkālattirunta tāyumāṉac celvarum,
aruḷā leviyumpā reṉṟāṉ-atti
	aṟiyātē cuṭṭiyeṉ ṉaṟivālē pārttēṉ
  iruḷāṉa poruḷkaṇṭa tallāṟ-kaṇṭa
	eṉṉiyuṅ kaṇṭileṉ eṉṉēṭi tōḻi.
eṉṟaruḷic ceytār. yāṉeṉa teṉṉun taliyeṭup puṭiyavarkaḷukku ulakattavar nēcattip peṟutalum iyalātirukkiyil, atic ciṟitēṉum uṭiyavarkaḷ avviruḷukkuḷ maṟinta oḷipōṟ pulappaṭātu niṟkum iṟivaṉi yāṅṅaṉam kāṇpar? aṉpiṉāl akaṅkari vāriṭattu ettakiya iṟumāppum illāmiyiṉ, avarpāl ellārum aṉpu nikaḻap peṟukiṉṟaṉar. innikaḻcciyō ṭoppavē ellām valla iṟivaṉum aṉpuṭiyārkku maṭṭumē aṇukkaṉāy niṉṟu aruḷ puriyum nīrmiyaṉāvaṉ. ivvāṟṟāl, aṉpunili yoṉṟē tavaniliyiṉum mēṟ paṭṭatākum. aṉpuniliyoṉṟē tavanilikku meṭ ṭāta mutalvaṉit taṉṉakap paṭuttu vatākum. tavaniliyō muyaṟci yuṭiyatu. aṉpuniliyō muyaṟciyaṟṟatu muyaṟci yuṭiyār muyaṉṟa poruḷip peṟuvatu iyalpāyirukkat tavamuyaṟci yuṭiyār kaṭavuḷi yeytāmiyum attakiya muyaṟci yillāta aṉparāyiṉār āṇṭavaṉik kāṇṭalum viyakkaṟ pālaṉavā mallavō? āyiṉum muyaṟciyāṟ peṟappaṭu vaṉa vellām kālatti ṉelliyilum iṭattiṉelli yilum akappaṭṭa varam puṭip poruḷkaḷāy aḻiyun taṉmiya vāy iruttalāl, kālattik kaṭantum iṭattik kaṭantum, aḻiviṉṟiyum, māṟutaliṉṟiyum varampilaṉāy viḷaṅkāniṉṟa mutalvaṉi attakiya muyaṟcikaḷ ettuṇic ciṟantaṉavā yiruppiṉum avi yāṅṅaṉañ ceṉṟu paṟṟum? maṟṟu, aṉpō attakiya muyaṟciyiṉ pāṟ paṭivatiṉṟik kālat tāṉum iṭattāṉum variyaṟukkap paṭātatāy aḻiviṉṟi māṟiṉṟi eṉṟum orupaṭittāy niṟintu niṟkumvaḻi, anniliyil taṉṉōṭotta iṟivaṉi atu tāṉ peṟṟu iṉpuṟutalum iyaṟki yākum. ip peṟṟittākiya pēraṉ pāl viḻuṅkap peṟṟavar evvakic ceyalumiṉṟi evvaki muyaṟciyumiṉṟip peruñ cōmparāyk kiṭappiṉum avarē iṟivaṉṟaṉ aruṭperu viḷakkaṅkaḷellām eḷitiṟ kāṇpar eṉpatu terippār,
cōmpar iruppatu cutta veḷiyilē
  cōmpar kiṭappatu cutta veḷiyilē
  cōmpar uṇarvu curuti muṭintiṭam
  cōmpar kaṇṭārac curutikkaṭ ṭūkkamē
eṉṟum,
tūṅkik kaṇṭārciva lōkamun tammuḷḷē
 tūṅkik kāṇṭār civa yōkamun tammuḷḷē
 tūṅkik kaṇṭārciva pōkamun tammuḷḷē
 tūṅkik kaṇṭārnili colva tevvāṟē.
eṉṟun teyvat tirumūlarum aruḷic ceytār. ākavē, tava muyaṟciyil niṉṟa muṉivararkkum pulaṉākāta mutalvaṉ attava muyaṟcikkum mēmpaṭṭa pēraṉpiṉ perukkāṉa māṇikka vācakap perumāṉāli ḻukkappaṭṭu avarkku eḷi vantu aruḷpurintatu peritum poruttamē yāmaṉṟō? ellām valla iṟivaṉ iṅṅaṉamāka nāṉmukaṉ mutalāṉa ciṟanta tēvarkaḷukkun tavamuyaṟciyiṟ ciṟanta muṉivarkaḷukkum eṭṭāta aruvat taṉmiyaṉā yiruntum, taṉṉi aṉpāṟ kuḻintu kuḻinturukum meyyaṭiyārkku mika eḷiyaṉāy uruvu koṇṭu vantu aruḷum iyalpiṉaṉ eṉpati nam camayāciriyar meyvaralāṟukaḷil maṭṭumē aiyamiṉṟit tuṇiyap peṟukiṉṟōm. nam camayāciriyarkaḷuḷḷun tiruñāṉa campantap perumāṉukkum māṇikkavācakap perumāṉukkum iṟivaṉ taṉ uṇmiyuruvil vantu aruḷ ceytamiyē mikavum pōṟṟaṟpālatoṉṟāyirukkiṉṟatu. ulakiyalaṟivu nirampap peṟāta mūṉṟāṇṭuc ciṟu kuḻantiyāyirunta pōtē tiruñāṉa campantap perumāṉ iṟivaṉiyum iṟiviyiyum nērēkaṇṭu avar tammāl aruṭpāl ūṭṭap peṟṟu ellām valla ñāṉāciriyarāyt tikaḻntu tām peṟṟa av varuḷamiḻtattic centamiḻt teyvap pākkaḷil nirappi avi vāyilāka nammaṉōrellām avvaruḷamiḻtattip paruki uyyac ceytaṉaraṉṟō? aṅṅaṉamē māṇikkavācakap perumāṉun tām amiccarāyirunta kālatti iṟivaṉiyum iṟiviyiyum nērēkaṇṭu avar tammāl āṭkoḷḷap paṭṭaṉareṉpatu,
karuvāy ulakiṉukku appuṟamāy ippuṟattē
 maruvār malarkkuḻaṉ mātiṉoṭum vantaruḷi
 aruvāy maṟipayil antaṇaṉā yāṇṭukoṇṭa
 tiruvāṉa tēvaṟkē ceṉṟūtāy kōttumpi.
 nāṉumeṉ cintiyu nāyakaṉuk kevviṭattōm
  tāṉuntaṉ tiyalun tāḻcaṭiyō ṉāṇṭilaṉēl  
   vāṉun ticikaḷu mākaṭalu māyapirāṉ
   ṟēṉuntu cēvaṭikkē ceṉṟūtāy kōttumpī.
eṉṟu avartāmē aruḷic ceyta arumit tirup pāṭṭukkaḷāl iṉitu viḷaṅkā niṟkiṉṟatu.
ivvāṟu kaṭavuḷi nērē kaṇṭatum, kaṇṭa akkāṭci yiṉik kaṇṭavartāmē teḷitteṭuttuc collutalum nam āciriyaṉ māriṭattaṉṟi maṟṟac camaya kuravarkaḷ pāṟ ciṟituṅ kāṇṭa liyalātu. nam āciriyaṉmār kaṭavuḷi nērēkaṇṭa aruṅkāṭcikaḷē uṇmiyāṉavi. ivvāṟākavum āriyar vaḻippaṭṭa pārppaṉarkaḷ kaṭavuḷ atō avarukku nērē vantu aruḷ purintār. itō ivarukku nērē vantu aruḷ purintār eṉṉum poyyāṉa purāṇakkatikaḷ palavaṟṟik kaṭṭiviṭṭu avitammāl nam camayāciriyar kaṭavuḷik kaṇṭa aruṅkāṭcikaḷiyum mika eḷiyaṉavākavuñ ciṟappillātaṉa  vākavum palar karutumpaṭi ceytu viṭṭaṉar.
eṉṟālum, nammāciriyaṉmār kaṇṭa teyvak kāṭcikku avartam aruṭcem pāṭalkaḷilēyē meyccāṉṟukaḷ iruttal pōlap purāṇakatikaḷiṟ kaṭavuḷik kaṇṭavarākac collappaṭuvōr taṅ kāṭcikaḷukku meyccāṉṟukaḷ ciṟitumilliyeṉpati uṇmiyārāycci ceypavarkaḷ teḷintu koḷḷal vēṇṭum. māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ evvaḷavu uṟutiyāka evvaḷavu uṇmiyāka iṟivaṉum iṟiviyun tamakkuk kāṭciyaḷittati mēlic ceyyuṭkaḷil eṭuttukkāṭṭi, empōlvārkkuṅ kaṭavuḷiṉ eḷivanta aruṭṭaṉmiyi viḷakkiyirukkiṉṟār!
iṉi, māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ kaṇṭa itteyvak kāṭciyāṉatu ataṉik kāṇṭalil vēṭki mītūrntu viḻippāyirunta ēṉi ettiṟattārkkum eṭṭātāyiṟ ṟeṉṟum, aṅṅaṉam viḻippāyiruntārkku uḷḷa muyaṟciyun takutip pāṭuñ ciṟitumillāt tamakku at teyvak kāṭci tōṉṟuvatāyiṟṟeṉṟum, avvāṟu tōṉṟiya atteyvak kāṭciyāṟ piṟarevarkkuṅ kāṭṭappaṭāta aruṭperum putumikaḷellām, piṟarevaruṅ kēḷāta arumaṟip poruḷkaḷellān tamakkuk kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa, collap paṭṭaṉa eṉṟum,
nāṭṭārkaḷ viḻittirukka ñālat tuḷḷē
	nāyiṉukkut taviciṭṭu nāyiṉēṟkē
  kāṭṭā taṉavellāṅ kāṭṭip piṉṉuṅ
	kēḷā taṉavellāṅ kēṭpit teṉṉi
  mīṭṭēyum piṟavāmaṟ kāttāṭ koṇṭā
	ṉemperumāṉ ceytiṭṭa vicci tāṉē.
eṉṉuñ coṟṟoṭarkaḷāl naṉkeṭuttu aruḷic ceytirukkiṉṟār.
iṉṉum, ittiruppāṭṭiṉkaṇ nāyiṉukkut 
taviciṭṭu nāyiṉēṟkē, kāṭṭātaṉavellāṅ kāṭṭi eṉṟu aṭikaḷ tammi nāyiṉukku oppiṭṭuc colli iruppataṉ nuṭpamum uṟṟu nōkkaṟpālatākum. ettuṇiyum periya iṟivaṉ ettuṇiyuñ ciṟiya makkaṭkut tāṉē valiyavantu taṉatu pēraruḷi vaḻaṅkum pērirakkattiṉ iyalpiyum ap pēraruḷip peṟuvārāṉa makkaḷiṉ piṟappiḻipiyum naṉku viḷaṅka vittaṟ poruṭṭu makkaḷi nāyppiṟavikku oppiṭu vārāyiṉār. nāyppiṟaviyiṉiṭattu naṉṟiyaṟita lākiya oru ciṟanta iyalpu iruppiṉum, ataṉiṭattuk kāṇappaṭum maṟṟip pala iyalpukaḷ mika iḻintaṉa vāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. ellā uyirkkum paciyuṅ kāmamum iyalpākavē amintuḷḷaṉa; eṉṟāluñ, cila ciṟṟuyirkaḷ appaciyit tīrttuk koḷḷum muṟiyiluṅ kāmattit tīrttuk koḷḷum muṟiyilum aṉpum nākarika oḻukkamum vāyntaṉavāyirukkiṉṟaṉa; maṟṟiccila uyirkaḷō attakiya aṉpum nākarikamum vāyātaṉa vāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivvakiyiṟ kākkiyiṉ naṭiyāṉatu mikavum pārāṭṭaṟ pālatākum. yāṅṅaṉameṉiṉ, oru kākkiyāṉatu taṉatu koṭumpaciyiṉit tīrkkum iriyiṉik kaṇṭaviṭattum, aḵtu ataṉit tāṉākavē uṇṇātu! taṉṉiṉamāṉa maṟṟik kākkikaḷiyuṅ kūvi yaḻittu avaṟṟōṭu oruṅkiruntu ataṉi uṇṇāniṟkum. ivvāṟu taṉ iṉattōṭuṇṇum icciṟanta iyaṟkiyi ēṉip palluyirkaḷiṭattuk kāṇṭal aritiṉum aritu. iṅṅaṉamē, taṉ kāmattit tīrkka atu taṉ tuṇi yōṭaṉṟip puṇarātum, aṅṅaṉam puṇaruṅkālum piṟavuyir kaḷiṉ kaṇṇiṟ paṭātum oḻukum iyaṟkiyiyum piṟa ciṟṟuyir kaḷiṭattuk kāṇṭal aritu. āṉāl nāyp piṟaviyilō ittakiya aṉpum ittakiya nākarikamuṅ kāṇappaṭutal ciṟitumilli. oru nāy taṉakkuk kiṭitta ecciṟ cōṟṟit tāṉ virintu kuvi uṇṇuṅkāliyil, taṉ maruṅkē piṟanāykaḷic ciṟitum aṇukaviṭāmal uṟumum; allatu avi aṇukumāyiṉ, avaṟṟic caṇṭiyiṭṭut turattum. ivviḻinta iyaṟkiyōṭu, āṇum peṇṇumāyp puṇaruṅ kālattum, palaruṅkāṇap puṇarntu tuṉpuṟṟu ciṟuvarkaḷāla likkappaṭutaluṅ kāṇkiṉṟōm. mēlum, nāyppiṟavi yāṉatu eḷitilē coriyum puṇṇuṅkoṇṭu puḻuttu aḻivatāyum uḷḷatu. iṅṅaṉamāka makkaḷiṉun tāḻnta ciṟṟuyirp piṟavikaḷuḷ nāyppiṟaviyiṉum iḻintatoṉ ṟaṉik kāṇṭal yāṇṭum iyalātu.
āṉāl āṟaṟivu vāynta makkaṭpiṟavi eṭuttāruṭ perumpālārō nāyppiṟaviyiṟ kāṇappaṭum iḻinta iyaṟki mūṉṟum uṭiyavarāyirukkiṉṟaṉar. tamakkuk kiṭitta uṇavit tām uṇṇa nēruṅkālattiṟ pacittuvarum ēṉiyōrit tampāl aṇukaviṭāmaṟ katavaṭittuk koṇṭu tāmākavē iruntuṇkiṉṟaṉar. orō orukāl tamakkuriya cuṟṟattavarōṭu uṭaṉiruntu avar uṇṇutaliyuṅ kāṇkiṉṟōmē eṉṟāl, makkaṭ piṟavi eṭuttārellām orē vakiyāṉa uṭampiṉamippum orēvakiyāṉa pakuttaṟivuṇarcciyum uṭiyarāyirukka, apperun tokiyiṉaril orucilarit tamakkuc cuṟṟattava reṉṟum, ēṉippalarit tamakkuc cuṟṟamallātavareṉṟum, oru cilari uyarnta cātiyāreṉṟum, ēṉippalari iḻinta cātiyāreṉṟun, tamakkuḷḷē palapirivukaḷiyum, pala vēṟṟumikaḷiyun tāmākavē kaṟpittuk koṇṭu, uyarnta nōkkamum uyarnta ceyalumiṉṟi veṟuñ cōṟṟup pēccup pēcip peruñ cōṟṟuccaṇṭiyiṭutaliyum, ataṉāl oṉṟukūṭi uḻit talum potu naṉmikkākap pāṭupaṭutalum ilarāy vāṇāḷi vīṇāḷāyk kaḻittu māḷkiṉṟaṉa rātaliṉ, aṉpillā ittakiyamakkaṭ piṟavi nāyppiṟaviyō ṭoppa iḻintatāta lik kāṇkiṉṟaṉa mallamō?
iṅṅaṉamē avarkaḷ kāmappacitīrkka muyalum muyaṟci yilum oru peṇṉi avaḷukkēṟṟa kaṇavaṉoṭu poruttā malum, ōrāṇi avaṉukkēṟṟa maṉiviyoṭu poruttā malun, tattam iṉattilēyē tattañ cuṟṟattilēyē peṇ koḷḷaluṅ koṭuttalum vēṇṭumeṉat tāntāmē kaṟpittuk koṇṭa pōlik kaṭṭuppāṭṭāṟ, kaṟṟuvalla ōr iḷiñaṉukkuk kallātavaḷoruttiyiyuṅ, kaṟṟuvalla oruttikkuk kallātāṉoruvaṉiyum, aḻakumikka oruvaṉukku aḻakillāḷoruttiyiyum, aḻakumikka oruttikku aḻakil lāṉ oruvaṉiyum, pētip paruvac ciṟumikkuk kātalillāk kiḻavaṉiyum piṇittuviṭṭu avar tammiyellāñ cākumaḷavum nāypōṟ caṇṭiyiṭṭu nalamiḻantu niṟkac ceykiṉṟaṉarkaḷallarō? iṉṉum palar piṟarkku aṭimiyāki avariṭṭa ecciṟ cōṟṟiyuṇṭum, avarporuṭṭu avarkkup pakiyāyiṉā roṭu pōr ceytu avarkaḷic cennīr oḻuka maṭittum nāyp piṟaviyōṭoppa nāṭkaḻikkiṉ ṟār kaḷallarō? iṉṉum paṟpalar piṟavuyirkaḷiṉ tuṉpat tic ciṟituṅ karutip pārātu, avvuyirkaḷik koṉṟu avvāṟṟāl varum ūṉiyuṇṭu uṇṭu vaṉṉeñcarāy vāṇāṭ kaḻikkiṉṟaṉa rallarō? mēlum palar tamakkiyinta maṉiviyarōṭu aṉpāl aḷavaḷāvi vāḻātu vari turiyiṉṟi vēci yariyum piṟariyum kūṭikkaḷittup puṇṇum paṭiyun toḻunōyuṅ koṇṭu puḻuttaḻikiṉṟaṉa rallarō? ivvāṟellām makkaṭ piṟaviyeṭuttār appiṟavip pēṟṟip pāḻākki nāyp piṟaviyōṭoppa nalaṉiḻantu niṟṟali nām nam kaṇṇetirē kāṇkiṉṟaṉa mākaliṉ, ittakiya makkaṭ piṟaviyāṉatu nāyp piṟavi yōṭoppa iḻintatātal kaṇṭē, nam perumāṉ māṇikka vācakar immakkaṭ piṟaviyi nāyppiṟaviyōṭoppumip paṭuttik kūṟuvārāyiṉar.
aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiyaviṭattum, attaṉmiyarāṉa makkaḷit tām iḻittup pēcutaṟku maṉam poruntārāy, avarkaḷiṉ iyaṟkiyit tammēlēṟṟit tammiyē iḻittup pēci avar pāṭiyiruppatu mikavum pārāṭṭaṟ pālatāyirukkiṉṟatu. kaṭavuḷiṭattu meyyaṉpu pūṇṭu oḻukum aruḷāḷarkaḷ ellā uyirkaḷiṭattum ellā makkaḷiṭattum--avarkaḷ ettuṇi iḻintōrāyiṉum allatu ettuṇi uyarntō rāyiṉum avarkaḷpāl viruppu veṟuppuk koḷḷātu, aṉpum irakkamumē koṇṭu oḻukuvareṉpataṟku ītu oru peruñ cāṉṟāy viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. māṇikkavācakar makkaḷu  ṭampil tōṉṟiṉārāyiṉum avar ēṉi makkaḷip pōlātu, am makkaḷiṉ vēṟāṉa taṉip peruñciṟappun taṉipperun teyvamāṭciyum uṭiyareṉpatu avararuḷic ceyta tiruvācakatti oru ciṟitu uṟṟu nōkkuvārkkum naṉkupulaṉām. māṇikkamuṅ kūḻāṅkalluṅ kalvaṭivil ottiruppiṉum, māṇikkaṅ kūḻāṅkalliṉ vēṟāṉa taṉi oḷiyum aḻakum peruviliyum uṭittātal pōlavum, nekiḻntavaṭivil nīrun tēṉum ottiruppiṉun tēṉāṉatu nīruk killāta tīñcuviyun teḷivum niṟaṉu muṭittātal pōlavum, oḷiyuṭaṉ miṉṉum vāṉmīṉkaḷum muḻumatiyum oru ciṟitu oppumiyuṭiyavāyiṉum muḻumatiyāṉatu vāṉ mīṉkaḷukku illāk kuḷirnta taṉippēroḷiyum aḻaku muṭittāy viḷaṅkutal pōlavum, māṇikkavācakap peru māṉum ēṉimakkaḷukkillā aruṭpēroḷivīci aruḷ vicumpiṟ kuṟiyātu tikaḻum muḻumatiyamē āvareṉpatu tiṇṇam. ellāmvalla civattikkaṇṭu civamāyē amarnta ipperuntakiyārē tammōṭotta makkaḷuṭampilirukkum uyirkaḷi oruciṟitum iḻittup pēcutaṟku maṉam orup paṭutalillārāy avarkkuḷḷa kuṟipāṭukaḷi yellān tammēlēṟṟik koṇṭu, iṟivaṉik kuṟiyirantu aḻutaḻutu pāṭāniṟka, ellāk kuṟṟaṅkaḷumuṭiya makkaḷuṭ   cilar ip peruntakiyāriyum ivari yotta maṟṟip periyārkaḷiyum, poṟumiyum nuṇṇaṟivuṅ koṇṭu ārāyntu pārkkum nalviṉiyum nallaṟivumilarāyt tamakkut tōṉṟiyavāṟellām ikaḻntu pēcip paḻipāvaṅkaḷukku āḷāy oḻikiṉṟaṉar. atu kiṭakka.
